Beyond that, we also contribute LargeFine-Person, a large-scale TBPS dataset with high-quality, fine-grained, and diverse textual annotations, enabling a practical and generalizable TBPS pre-training benchmark under unsupervised setting. Experiments on multiple TBPS benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and generalization of both GTR+ and LargeFine-Person. Code is available at: https://github.com/Flame-Chasers/GTR.

</details>


### [67] [SV-Cine: Diagnosis-Conditioned Segmentation of Single Ventricle Physiology via Generative Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12997)
*Lila Cunge,Yuehong Liu,Hang Xu,Thomas Coudert,Pierangelo Renella,J Paul Finn,William Hsu,Kim-Lien Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究面向单心室生理（SVP）这一罕见先心病，提出诊断条件化的心脏MRI分割框架：利用SDF4CHD生成合成3D心脏网格与合成MRI增强数据，并将基础模型CineMA通过FiLM层进行诊断条件化适配（SV-Cine），在心室腔与心肌分割上优于nnU-Net。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVP是罕见先心病亚型，仅存单功能心室且解剖构型不典型，传统图像分割方法难以适用；同时临床数据稀缺、SVP亚型间形态差异大，导致稳健分割方法开发困难。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：1）数据增强流水线，用SDF4CHD生成合成3D心脏网格，并通过生成式建模产生对应的合成心脏MRI；2）SV-Cine，即对基础模型CineMA进行诊断条件化适配，通过Feature-wise Linear Modulation（FiLM）层引入患者层级诊断信息，实现分割过程中诊断感知的特征自适应。

Result: 在包含不同SVP亚型的内部队列上评估：SV-Cine左心室Dice中位数0.89（IQR 0.80–0.91），右心室0.72（IQR 0.54–0.84），在右心室分割上比最强基线nnU-Net高出0.39 Dice点；优势心室射血分数误差中位数5.55个百分点（IQR 3.41–7.69）。相较内部队列，外部队列的LV与心肌分割性能较低，而RV Dice在两队列间相当。

Conclusion: 预训练基础模型可通过引入诊断先验适配到高度专业化的下游任务，同时利用大规模MRI预训练中学到的解剖知识，从而在数据稀缺、形态多样的SVP分割任务中取得良好表现。

Abstract: Single Ventricle Physiology (SVP) is a rare subtype of congenital heart disease characterized by the presence of a single functional cardiac ventricle with atypical anatomic configurations that challenge conventional image segmentation approaches. The scarcity of clinical data and the morphological diversity across SVP subtypes make the development of robust segmentation methods particularly difficult. To address these limitations, we propose a cardiac MRI segmentation framework focused on ventricular chambers and myocardium segmentation tailored for SVP. First, we introduce a data augmentation pipeline that generates synthetic 3D cardiac meshes using SDF4CHD and corresponding synthetic cardiac MRI through generative modeling. Second, we introduce SV-Cine, a diagnosis-conditioned adaptation of the foundation model CineMA that incorporates patient-level diagnostic information through Feature-wise Linear Modulation layers, enabling diagnosis-aware feature adaptation during segmentation. We evaluated the framework on an internal cohort with varying SVP subtypes. SV-Cine achieved median Dice scores of 0.89 (IQR: 0.80--0.91) for the left ventricle and 0.72 (IQR: 0.54--0.84) for the right ventricle, outperforming the strongest baseline, nnU-Net, by 0.39 Dice points on right ventricle segmentation. It also yields a median ejection fraction error of 5.55 percentage points (IQR: 3.41--7.69) for the dominant ventricle. Compared with the internal cohort, LV and myocardium segmentation performance was lower for the external cohort; whereas RV Dice scores were comparable for both cohorts. Our findings suggest that a pretrained foundation model can be adapted for highly specialized downstream tasks through usage of diagnosis priors while leveraging anatomic knowledge learned from large-scale MRI datasets during pretraining.

</details>


### [68] [Physics-Aware Video Generation via Agentic Planning and Graph-Guided Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13006)
*Minh-Loi Nguyen,Xuan-Vu Le,Thanh-Toan Do,Tam V. Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练引导框架PhysPlan，用VLM作为认知模拟器生成运动学轨迹与3D深度表征，并通过物体级梯度路由与动力学强度剖析实现物理感知的视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型（VDM）虽能生成高保真视频，但缺乏对物理规律的内在理解，常产生结构幻觉与物理不合理的动态。现有免训练测试时优化方法依赖全局梯度更新与刚性调度启发式，会破坏被动背景且无法建模复杂动态状态变化。

Method: PhysPlan将范式从随机视觉插值转变为智能体物理模拟：1）VLM作为迭代式认知模拟器，将多模态输入分解为视觉思维链（Chain-of-Visual-Thought），构建运动学轨迹与3D深度几何的多模态表征；2）以物体为中心的测试时优化，引入Object-Centric Gradient Routing隔离运动学修改并锁定被动环境；3）Kinetic Intensity Profiling动态参数化框架超参，以适应不同程度的物理形变。

Result: 在PhyGenBench与Physics-IQ基准上的大量评测表明，PhysPlan显著优于基础VDM与可控VDM基线。

Conclusion: PhysPlan为提升视频生成的物理理解能力提供了一条有前景的免训练引导路径。

Abstract: Video diffusion models (VDMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in synthesizing high-fidelity, photorealistic video content. However, they fundamentally lack an intrinsic understanding of physical laws and frequently produce visually appealing but causally illogical sequences characterized by structural hallucinations and physically implausible dynamics. Injecting physical awareness via training-free test-time optimization is a promising alternative, yet existing methods rely on global gradient updates and rigid scheduling heuristics that inadvertently corrupt passive backgrounds and fail to model complex dynamic state changes. To address this, we propose PhysPlan, a novel training-free guidance framework that shifts the paradigm from stochastic visual interpolation to agentic physics simulation. First, a VLM operates as an iterative cognitive simulator, decomposing multimodal inputs into a Chain-of-Visual-Thought to create a multimodal representation of kinematic trajectories and 3D depth geometries. Second, these signals drives an object-centric test-time optimization. Unlike prior training-free methods that rely on global gradients and rigid scheduling heuristics, PhysPlan introduces Object-Centric Gradient Routing to isolate kinematic modifications and completely lock the passive environment. Furthermore, our Kinetic Intensity Profiling dynamically parameterizes framework hyperparameters to accommodate the varying severity of physical deformations. Extensive evaluations on the PhyGenBench and Physics-IQ benchmarks demonstrate that PhysPlan significantly outperforms both foundational and controllable VDM baselines, offering a promising approach for improving the physical understanding of video generation.

</details>


### [69] [TileNet: Tile-Based CNN-SVM Architecture for Autonomous Unmanned Aerial Systems Inspection of Flat Roofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13013)
*Samuel Dunthorne,Hashim A. Hashim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于无人机（UAS）的实时深度学习框架，通过双高度飞行与轻量化CNN-SVM分类器在嵌入式硬件上实现平屋顶缺陷的自主检测，测试准确率达94.4%，优于GoogLeNet与AlexNet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平屋顶是建筑围护结构中最具影响的部件之一，其性能直接关系到能耗、碳排放与环境可持续性。及时检测屋顶缺陷对于减少冷热损失、防止潮湿导致的霉变等退化、以及支撑气候减缓目标至关重要。传统人工检测风险高、成本大，因此需要一种快速、可重复且安全的自动化检测方案。

Method: 提出一种基于无人机系统的实时深度学习框架：采用双高度航拍的多分辨率飞行策略以兼顾细尺度缺陷与大型结构问题；为满足嵌入式硬件的算力与功耗约束，采用基于分块（tile-based）架构与轻量化CNN-SVM分类器实现低延迟机载推理。最终模型包含5个卷积层与4个全连接层，最后一层为线性SVM头。

Result: 在照片级划分的数据集（43,383张训练、3,869张验证、2,540张测试的分块与增强图像）上，平均测试准确率为94.4%（三个随机种子下95%置信区间±0.4%），优于GoogLeNet（89.2%）与AlexNet（79.8%）。使用DJI Matrice 350 RTK无人机现场采集的真实影像进行的实验表明，系统可支持快速、可重复且安全的屋顶巡检。

Conclusion: 该框架能够降低人工风险与运营成本，实现更可持续的建筑维护，为屋顶缺陷的实时自动化检测提供了一种高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Flat roofs are among the most influential components of the building envelope, governing both structural performance and thermal efficiency, and thereby contributing directly to household energy consumption, carbon emissions, and long-term environmental sustainability. Timely detection of roof defects is essential for reducing heating and cooling losses, preventing moisture-driven degradation such as mold growth, and supporting national climate-change mitigation goals. This paper presents a real-time, Unmanned Aerial System (UAS)-based deep learning framework that autonomously detects defects using live imagery captured during dual-altitude aerial passes. The multi-resolution flight strategy is designed to aid the identification of both small, fine-scale defects and larger structural issues, enabling more comprehensive assessments. To meet the strict computational and power constraints of embedded UAS hardware, the proposed framework integrates a tile-based architecture with a lightweight Convolution Neural Network-Support Vector Machine (CNN-SVM) classifier designed for low-latency onboard inference. The final model-comprising five convolutional layers and four dense layers, the last a linear SVM head, achieved a mean test accuracy of $94.4\%$ ($95\%$ confidence interval $\pm0.4\%$ over three seeds) on a photo-level split ($43,383$ training, $3,869$ validation, and $2,540$ test tiled and augmented images), outperforming GoogLeNet ($89.2\%$) and AlexNet ($79.8\%$). Experimental evaluations using real UAS imagery collected by onsite visits with DJI Matrice 350 RTK drone demonstrate that the system supports rapid, repeatable, and safe roof inspections while reducing human risk, lowering operational costs, and enabling more sustainable building maintenance.

</details>


### [70] [Label-Guided Knowledge Distillation for 3D-CNNs in Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13024)
*Yanjiang Shi,Peng Zhao,Nan Qi,Guiqin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出标签引导知识蒸馏（LGKD），通过样本级与类别级蒸馏，指导3D-CNN学生模型学习教师特征，在UCF101和HMDB51上取得有竞争力结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数视频分析中的特征蒸馏方法只是图像分析方法的简单移植，忽视了视频特征在时间维度上的差异，导致蒸馏效果受限。

Method: 提出LGKD，利用真实标签引导学生特征蒸馏，包含两部分：1）样本级蒸馏，利用标签信息与教师概率分布引导对时间精度影响显著的特征学习，同时抑制噪声；2）类别级特征蒸馏，采用原型网络捕获同类样本间的关系知识，增强学生模型对高维语义信息的学习与泛化能力。

Result: 在两个基准动作识别数据集UCF101和HMDB51上进行了全面实验，取得有竞争力的结果，验证了方法的有效性与优越性。

Conclusion: LGKD通过标签引导的样本级与类别级特征蒸馏，弥补了视频特征时间维度差异带来的不足，提升了轻量学生模型在动作识别任务上的性能。

Abstract: As a key model compression technique, knowledge distillation aims to transfer knowledge from a high-capacity teacher model to a lightweight student model for enhancing the latter's performance. In this work, we reviewed the feature knowledge distillation for 3D-CNNs and observed that most feature distillation methods in video analysis are simple adaptations of those used in image analysis, often neglecting the differences of video features in the temporal dimension. To address this issue, we proposed Label-Guided Knowledge Distillation (LGKD) to guide the distillation of student model features using ground truth labels. Our method entails two components: sample-wise distillation and class-wise distillation, enabling the student model to learn feature representation of the teacher model at two levels. Sample-wise distillation utilizes label information and the teacher's probability distribution to guide the learning of features that significantly impact temporal accuracy while mitigating noise. Meanwhile, class-wise feature distillation employs a prototype network to further capture the relational knowledge among samples within the same category, enhancing the student's ability to learn higher-dimensional semantic information and improving model generalization. To demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of our method, we conducted comprehensive experiments on two benchmark action recognition datasets, UCF101 and HMDB51, achieving competitive results.

</details>


### [71] [Unified CT and MRI Pancreas Segmentation for Label-Efficient Cross-Modality Subregion Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13043)
*Ziliang Hong,Hongyi Pan,Halil Ertugrul Aktas,Andrea Bejar,Elif Keles,Frank H. Miller,Michael B. Wallace,Rajesh N. Keswani,Gorkem Durak,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出统一的3D胰腺分割框架，通过域对抗学习在4,604例CT与MRI扫描上学习解剖表征，实现跨模态全胰腺分割及标签高效的下游子区域迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割在不同成像模态间面临外观与强度分布差异大的问题，单模态训练模型在未见域上性能显著下降，亟需跨模态鲁棒的解剖表征。

Method: 采用共享的nnU-Net编码器-解码器进行全胰腺分割，引入潜在域判别器进行域对抗学习以对齐CT-MRI特征；随后将学到的编码器迁移至胰头-胰体-胰尾子区域分割，仅使用有限的MRI子区域标注。

Result: 全胰腺分割在同分布测试集上平均Dice为87.31%，在外部OOD数据集上Dice为84.20%–88.09%；下游子区域分割在MRI上Dice为80.53%、CT上为83.05%，且未使用CT子区域标注。

Conclusion: 统一的解剖表征可同时支持跨模态胰腺分割与标签高效的下游迁移。

Abstract: Robust medical image segmentation across imaging modalities is challenging because of large differences in appearance and intensity distributions. Models trained on a single modality often show substantial performance drops when applied to unseen domains. In this work, we develop a unified 3D pancreas segmentation framework that applies domain-adversarial learning to 4,604 heterogeneous CT and MRI scans to learn anatomical representations. A shared nnU-Net encoder-decoder is trained for whole-pancreas segmentation, with a latent domain discriminator encouraging CT-MRI feature alignment. The learned encoder is subsequently transferred to pancreatic head-body-tail segmentation using limited MRI-only subregion annotations. An average Dice score of 87.31% on the in-distribution test set and Dice scores ranging from 84.20% to 88.09% across external OOD datasets were achieved in whole pancreas segmentation. Dice scores of 80.53% on MRI and 83.05% on CT were achieved for downstream subregion segmentation, without using CT subregion annotations. These results demonstrate that a unified anatomical representation can support both cross-modality pancreas segmentation and label-efficient downstream transfer.

</details>

### [72] [R2VC: Modular Fact-Checking with Retrieval, Verification, and Confidence Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11955)
*Dhruv Dixit,Paritosh Pandey*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出模块化事实核查架构 R2VC（检索-推理-验证-校准），在 FEVER 上以 8B 模型取得比基线高 13.74% 的准确率，验证器筛选与置信度校准贡献最大，检索失败（尤其是错误实体证据）仍是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端提示式自动事实核查将证据检索、推理与不确定性估计耦合在一起，导致失败难以诊断、置信度难以信任，因此需要解耦的模块化流程以提升可解释性与可靠性。

Method: R2VC 由四个模块构成：(1) 对 Wikipedia 进行稀疏+稠密混合检索；(2) 经监督微调并以 DPO 对齐的生成器产出多样化的结构化判定候选；(3) 外部 NLI 交叉编码器基于证据进行候选选择；(4) 轻量级序列级校准器用于置信度估计与选择性弃答，输出带引用与弃答的结论。

Result: 在 FEVER 上，8B 骨干配合 R2VC 比基线准确率高 13.74%；消融显示移除候选选择后准确率降至 76.24%，移除校准后 Brier 分数近乎翻倍至 0.161；对 250 个错误的人工分析表明检索失败（尤其错误实体证据）是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 验证器驱动的候选选择与置信度校准是性能提升的主要来源，模块化事实核查流水线能在开放域验证中同时显著提升预测准确率与置信度可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for automated fact checking, but end-to-end prompting often entangles evidence retrieval, reasoning, and uncertainty estimation, making failures difficult to diagnose and confidence difficult to trust. We present R2VC, a modular retrieve, reason, verify, calibrate architecture for evidence-grounded fact checking with citations and abstention. R2VC combines hybrid sparse+dense retrieval over Wikipedia, a supervised fine-tuned and DPO-aligned generator that produces diverse structured verdict candidates, an external NLI cross-encoder for evidence-based candidate selection, and a lightweight sequence-level calibrator for confidence estimation and selective abstention. On FEVER, an 8B backbone with R2VC achieves 13.74% higher accuracy than baseline. Ablation studies show that verifier-based candidate selection and confidence calibration are the largest contributors to performance. Removing candidate selection drops FEVER accuracy to 76.24%, while removing calibration nearly doubles the Brier score to 0.161. A manual analysis of 250 errors further shows that retrieval failures, especially wrong-entity evidence, remain the dominant bottleneck. Together, these results show that modular fact-checking pipelines can substantially improve both predictive accuracy and confidence reliability in open-domain verification.

</details>


### [73] [What Counts as a Mistake? Annotating Recitation Events in Quran Memorization Transcripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12085)
*Mohamad Al Mdfaa,Nursultan Askarbekuly,Ahmed Helaly,Ubai Sandouk,Manuel Mazzara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文报告了古兰经诵读ASR转录中错误判别的100例人工标注（348个评分单元、162个局部事件、10类标签），并构建可执行评测器，对比朴素diff与生产系统组件两种基线的标签感知与定位性能，初步试验显示编码智能体显著优于基线，瓶颈主要在标注约定而非检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古兰经诵读核查需从ASR转录中区分未解决的错误、重复、修正、开场套语与可接受的拼写差异，该任务缺乏公开的人工标注数据与联合评测手段，且现有流水线基线的失败原因（如坐标错误）未被系统诊断。

Method: 完成100例录音的十类标签人工标注，得到348个评分单元与162个局部事件；设计可执行的评测器，同时评分标签与词位置；设置两类基线：朴素diff与适配后的生产清洗/对齐组件；进行初步试验，8次单轮20分钟运行，覆盖3个编码智能体与8个模型，并与六次同日运行结果对比。

Result: 朴素diff的标签感知F1为0.525、定位F1为0.826；生产组件为0.518和0.786，两者精确跨度F1均为0.505；修正适配器词坐标后恢复了全部5个重复事件。试验中标签感知F1在0.143至0.892之间，7个运行远高于全部基线，1个因缺失归一化步骤低于朴素diff；六次运行中972个金标事件实例有970个获得重叠预测；7/162事件未被任何同日运行命中，其中5个源于同一条正字法规则。

Conclusion: 剩余难点不在事件检测，而在标注约定——跨度范围与由裁决而非文本可见性界定的标签；标注接口必须先于基线失败的解释加以校验；该试验仅衡量任务的算法部分，未进行构建前标注。

Abstract: Checking Quran recitation from an ASR transcript requires distinguishing unresolved mistakes from repetitions, repairs, opening formulas and accepted spelling differences. We report a completed human annotation of 100 production recording cases: 348 scored units and 162 localized events across ten combined labels. An executable evaluator scores labels and word positions together. A plain diff reaches label-aware F1 0.525 and localization F1 0.826; adapted production cleaner/alignment components reach 0.518 and 0.786, with exact-span F1 0.505 for both. Correcting the adapter's word coordinates recovers all five annotated repetition events, showing why annotation interfaces must be checked before interpreting baseline failures. In a preliminary pilot, eight single 20-minute runs across three coding agents and eight models span label-aware F1 0.143 to 0.892: seven land far above every baseline, and one collapses below the naive diff from a missing normalization step. Across the six, 970 of 972 gold-event instances draw an overlapping prediction, so what remains is not detection but convention: span extent, and the labels whose boundary is stipulated by adjudication rather than visible in the text. Seven of 162 events defeat all six same-day runs, five of them one orthographic rule, and the strongest run still misses the same ones. No run annotated before building, so the pilot measures the algorithm half of the task only.

</details>


### [74] [Extracting Dataset Mentions in Forced Displacement and FCV Documents: A Weakly Supervised Framework with LLM-Based Label Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12107)
*Rafael Macalaba,Aivin V. Solatorio,Patrick Michael Brock,Olivier Dupriez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种弱监督框架，将数据集提及抽取迁移至被迫流离失所与FCV领域：先用通用文献上训练的轻量模型从未标注领域文档中生成候选提及，由前沿LLM在上下文中审核校正，再辅以合成与对比样本微调轻量模型，实现大规模抽取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展与人道组织产出大量调查、行政登记等数据资源，但其被引用的位置散落在研究论文、项目文档、人道报告中，难以系统识别，既妨碍数据使用追溯，也难发现数据可得性或传播方面的缺口；而领域内缺乏大规模人工标注语料。

Method: 弱监督框架：1) 在通用研究文献上训练轻量模型，对未标注的目标领域文档生成候选数据集提及；2) 前沿LLM在上下文中审核候选，进行验证/拒绝并修正抽取边界；3) 将所得标注补充针对性合成与对比样本，用于微调轻量模型以支持大规模抽取。评测基于覆盖研究、人道与业务文档的1,706段独立金标准基准。

Result: 全基准上提及级精确率74.1%、召回率70.5%；在含数据集引用的段落中精确率达89.5%；段落级判别（有/无数据集引用）准确率88.2%、特异度88.6%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在标注数据有限时构建领域专用监督是可行的实用路径，并为更大规模分析数据使用状况及流离失所数据格局中的潜在缺口提供了技术基础。

Abstract: Development and humanitarian organizations produce and support surveys, administrative registries, and other data resources to inform research, policy, and operations, yet systematically identifying where these datasets are referenced remains difficult. Such references are dispersed across research papers, project documents, humanitarian reports, and other unstructured text, limiting both the ability to trace data use and to identify potential gaps in data availability or dissemination. We present a weakly supervised framework for adapting dataset extraction to forced displacement and Fragile, Conflict, and Violence (FCV) documents without first constructing a large manually labeled training corpus. A lightweight model trained on general research literature generates candidate dataset mentions from unlabeled domain documents, which a frontier large language model (LLM) reviews in context, validating or rejecting candidates and correcting their extraction boundaries. The resulting annotations are supplemented with targeted synthetic and contrastive examples and used to fine-tune the lightweight model for large-scale extraction. We evaluate the resulting model on an independent gold-standard benchmark of 1,706 text passages spanning research, humanitarian, and operational documents. Across the full benchmark, the model achieves 74.1\% precision and 70.5\% recall at the mention level; among passages containing dataset references, precision reaches 89.5\%. At the passage level, the model achieves 88.2\% accuracy and 88.6\% specificity in distinguishing passages with dataset references from those without them. These results demonstrate a practical approach for constructing domain-specific supervision when labeled data are limited, and provide a technical foundation for larger-scale analysis of data use and potential gaps in the displacement data landscape.

</details>


### [75] [The Cost of Compression: A Rate-Distortion Limit on Factual Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12111)
*Xi Wang,Shijia Xu,Rongfeng Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出覆盖-压缩模型，证明事实幻觉除覆盖不足外还存在有限记忆导致的观测事实有损回忆误差，并给出误差下界及实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将闭卷问答中的事实幻觉归因于覆盖问题，即模型内部记忆缺失相关事实。但这一视角忽略了第二种误差来源：即使事实已被观测，有限记忆也可能迫使其仅被近似存储，从而导致有损回忆。作者希望刻画这一可分离的失败模式。

Method: 构建简单的覆盖-压缩事实回忆模型：非结构化问答任务含N个查询、K个答案，学习器观测M条训练事实并压缩至至多B比特，在无检索条件下回答均匀抽取的测试查询。针对均匀随机真实映射，推导误差下界；该下界中δ*(r)为均匀K元信源在零一损失下的率失真函数逆。随后用理论驱动的仿真和受控事实注入探针，在现代语言模型中改变事实负载与有效可训练记忆进行验证。

Result: 证明误差下界 E ≥ (M/N)δ*(B/M) + (1-M/N)(1-1/K)。其中首项刻画已观测事实上的压缩失真，次项刻画未观测事实上的覆盖缺失。该界为选择性记忆、强制压缩、结构、检索、弃答及长上下文组织提供了紧凑的分析框架，并于仿真和语言模型探针中呈现出可预测的特征。

Conclusion: 该工作并非完整的幻觉理论，而是从信息论角度刻画了一种可分离的失败模式：有限记忆下对已观测事实的有损回忆，并将压缩失真与覆盖缺失两个误差来源清晰分解。

Abstract: Factual hallucination in closed-book question answering is often treated as a coverage problem: a model fails because the relevant fact is absent from its internal memory. This view misses a second source of error. Even when a fact has been observed, finite memory may force it to be stored only approximately. We study this effect through a simple coverage--compression model of factual recall. We consider an unstructured question-answering task with $N$ possible queries and $K$ possible answers. A learner observes $M$ training facts, compresses them into at most $B$ bits, and answers uniformly drawn test queries without retrieval. For a uniformly random ground-truth mapping, we prove $\mathcal{E} \geq \frac{M}{N}δ^\star\!\left(\frac{B}{M}\right) + \left(1-\frac{M}{N}\right)\left(1-\frac{1}{K}\right)$, where $δ^\star(r)$ is the inverse rate-distortion function of a uniform $K$-ary source under zero-one loss. The two terms separate compression distortion on observed facts from missing coverage on unobserved facts. The bound gives a compact way to reason about selective memory, forced compression, structure, retrieval, abstention, and long-context organization. We study the predicted signatures with theory-implied simulations and controlled fact-injection probes in modern language models that vary fact load and effective trainable memory. The result is not a complete theory of hallucination, but an information-theoretic account of a separable failure mode: lossy recall of observed facts under finite memory.

</details>


### [76] [Quantifying Consonant Contributions to Word Intelligibility via Acoustic Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12122)
*Eunjung Yeo,Kwanghee Choi,Krupaben Kothadia,Visar Berisha,Julie M. Liss,David R. Mortensen,David Harwath*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于声学掩蔽的可扩展方法，通过逐一静音单词中的辅音并测试ASR模型能否识别，定义“掩蔽诱发误识率（MMR）”来量化辅音对可懂度的贡献，并在四种语言、三种ASR架构上验证其与音位频率和功能负荷的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅音对单词可懂度的贡献不均等，而治疗时间有限，因此按贡献对辅音排序有助于确定运动言语障碍的干预优先级。但传统上测量这种贡献依赖感知实验，难以规模化，故需要一种可扩展的测量方法。

Method: 在孤立词中逐一静音单个辅音，测试ASR模型是否仍能识别该词；将某辅音的贡献分数定义为被掩蔽后单词被误识的比例，即掩蔽诱发误识率（MMR）。以音位频率和功能负荷两个已有语言学因素对MMR进行验证。实验覆盖英语、西班牙语、德语、捷克语四种语言，以及MMS（仅编码器）、Whisper（编码器-解码器）、Qwen3-ASR（基于LLM）三种ASR架构，采用偏Spearman相关分析。

Result: 偏Spearman相关显示，音位频率与MMR呈负相关，功能负荷与MMR呈正相关：即更频繁的辅音被掩蔽时破坏性较小，而承载更多词汇对立的辅音被掩蔽时破坏性较大。跨语言分析进一步表明，各语言的辅音排序并不一致。

Conclusion: MMR是一种可扩展且有效的辅音贡献度量，其与已知语言学因素的相关模式验证了方法的合理性；辅音贡献具有语言依赖性，提示干预目标的排序需针对具体语言制定。

Abstract: Consonants contribute unequally to whether a word is understood. Given the limited time available for therapy, ranking consonants by contribution to intelligibility helps prioritize intervention targets in motor speech disorders. However, measuring this contribution relies on perceptual studies that are difficult to scale. This paper presents a scalable method that measures consonant contribution using acoustic masking. We silence one consonant at a time in an isolated word and test whether an automatic speech recognition (ASR) model still recognizes the word. We define a consonant's contribution score as the proportion of its masked instances for which the word becomes misrecognized, which we refer to as the mask-induced misrecognition rate (MMR). We validate MMR against two linguistic factors previously reported to correlate with consonant contribution, namely phoneme frequency and functional load. We apply this analysis across four languages, English, Spanish, German, and Czech, using three ASR architectures, MMS (encoder-only), Whisper (encoder-decoder), and Qwen3-ASR (LLM-based). Using partial Spearman correlations, we find that phoneme frequency correlates negatively with MMR while functional load correlates positively. In other words, more frequent consonants are less disruptive when masked, whereas consonants carrying more lexical contrast are more disruptive. Further cross-language analysis shows that consonant rankings are not consistent, indicating that consonant contribution is language-dependent.

</details>


### [77] [Local Edits, Global Ripples: Replay-Informed Policy Adaptation for Workflow Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12127)
*Manqing Mao,Hong Wang,Samson Koelle,Jie Yuan,Zhuoer Wang,James Feng,Yanjun Lin,Daniel Edmiston,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Zhecheng Sheng,Wei Niu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RIPPLE方法，将"编辑哪里"与"组合后是否安全"解耦，通过回放评估提示策略编辑的组合效应，在Flow-HO基准上验证成功率提升最高23.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化提示策略编辑面临两个耦合问题：编辑局部性不等于效果局部性（局部编辑可波及下游执行），以及编辑效果对组合敏感（单独有效的编辑组合后可能失效甚至有害）。因此需要同时支持两个决策：从执行反馈中确定策略应在何处修改，以及判断编辑在组合后是否仍可安全保留。

Method: RIPPLE（Replay-Informed Persistent Policy Localization and Editing）将"在哪里编辑"与"组合后是否安全"分离。首先诊断失败轨迹，将可操作的失败映射到预定义策略片段，将修正限制在该片段内；随后针对同一迭代起始策略评估候选编辑的孤立增益，再将有前景的编辑在已接受的更新之后回放，以暴露下游影响与编辑间交互；仅保留组合后仍安全的编辑。

Result: 在针对可执行工作流合成的合成留出基准Flow-HO上评估，验证成功率最高提升23.1%，并在两个额外的冻结语言模型骨干上取得正向增益，同时保持编辑效率与较低执行成本。定向交互分析进一步证实了上述两个性质：一个片段局部的工具使用编辑改变了下游资源解析与验证，而一个单独有益的编辑在组合后变得有害。

Conclusion: RIPPLE通过解耦编辑定位与组合安全性验证，有效缓解了持久化提示策略编辑中效果非局部性与组合敏感性问题，在不更新底层模型的前提下提升了工作流合成代理的性能，且具备跨骨干模型的泛化性与成本效率。

Abstract: Prompt-policy editing offers a practical way to improve agents that synthesize executable workflows without updating the underlying model. However, persistent prompt editing has two coupled properties. First, edit locality does not imply effect locality: an edit confined to one policy segment can ripple through downstream execution, altering behavior beyond the edited segment. Second, edit effects are composition-sensitive: edits that work in isolation can interfere after composition, causing one or both to lose their benefit or become harmful. Persistent adaptation must therefore support two distinct decisions: identifying where the policy should change from execution feedback, and determining whether the resulting edit remains safe to persist after composition.
  To address these challenges, we introduce RIPPLE (Replay-Informed Persistent Policy Localization and Editing), which separates where an edit is made from whether it remains safe after composition. It diagnoses failed trajectories, maps each actionable failure to a predefined policy segment, and restricts the correction to that part of the policy. RIPPLE then evaluates candidates against the same iteration-start policy to compare their isolated gains, before replaying promising edits after previously accepted updates to expose downstream effects and interactions. Only edits that remain safe under composition are retained.
  We evaluate RIPPLE on Flow-HO, a synthetic held-out benchmark for executable workflow synthesis. RIPPLE improves validation success by up to 23.1% and yields positive gains on two additional frozen language-model backbones, while maintaining edit efficiency and low execution cost. Targeted interaction analysis further demonstrates both properties: a segment-local tool-use edit changes downstream resource resolution and validation, while an edit beneficial in isolation becomes harmful after composition.

</details>


### [78] [Population-level measures of perceived food access reveal barriers beyond geographic proximity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12132)
*Teresa Groton,Benjamin rachunok*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究利用北卡罗来纳州罗利市49家杂货店的25,125条Google Maps评论，通过无监督主题建模与零样本分类，测量了食品可及性的五个维度，发现感知可及性可揭示地理邻近性无法捕捉的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人口层面的食品可及性测量仍高度依赖地理指标，因为感知维度难以大规模测量，而食品可及性本质上是多维的，仅靠地理邻近性无法全面反映居民与食品环境的匹配程度。

Method: 采集罗利市49家杂货店的25,125条Google Maps评论，聚焦可及性的五个维度：可得性、可达性、可负担性、适配性与可接受性。先用无监督主题建模识别评论主题，再用零样本分类将主题映射到各可及性维度，与人工编码的一致率为85.4%，并据此构建店铺层面的测量指标。

Result: 构建的店铺层面指标捕捉到食品可及性的不同侧面，揭示了单靠地理邻近性无法反映的障碍；同一连锁品牌邻近门店的比较显示，相同的店铺政策在不同地点被感知差异显著；感知可及性呈现系统性的社会经济与人口模式，与地理可及性模式大体平行但并不重合。

Conclusion: 在线杂货评论可作为地理可及性测量的可扩展补充手段，支持在人口尺度上刻画感知层面的食品可及性，反映居民与食品环境之间的匹配关系。

Abstract: Food access is multidimensional, but population-level measurement still relies heavily on geography because perceived dimensions of access are difficult to measure at scale. Here, we use 25,125 Google Maps reviews from 49 grocery stores in Raleigh, North Carolina, to measure five dimensions of food access: availability, accessibility, affordability, accommodation, and acceptability. We identify review topics with unsupervised topic modeling and assign them to access dimensions using zero-shot classification, with 85.4% agreement against manual coding. The resulting store-level measures capture distinct aspects of food access and reveal barriers that geographic proximity alone does not capture. Comparisons between nearby stores in the same chain further show that identical store policies can be perceived very differently across locations, consistent with food access reflecting the fit between residents and their food environment. Perceived food access also follows systematic socioeconomic and demographic patterns that broadly parallel, but do not replicate, those observed for geographic access. These results show that online grocery reviews can provide a scalable complement to geographic measures of food access.

</details>


### [79] [GAUGE: When Not to Trust LLM-as-a-Judge in User-Simulated Evaluation of Task-Oriented Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12191)
*Umesh Bodhwani,Thanh Tran,Kai Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GAUGE离线评估协议，检验"人格化LLM用户模拟+LLM裁判"这一低成本评估门控的排名是否与可验证奖励一致，发现满意度与任务成功脱钩（57.5%满意对话实际失败），且门控在强代理间丧失分辨率（决策分歧率由<1%升至31%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务型LLM代理的选型日益依赖低成本离线评估门控：LLM用户模拟器对话、LLM裁判打分、高分者晋级。但该门控是否能反映真实任务成功尚不明确，且已有实践混淆了排名效度与构念效度两类评估有效性。

Method: 提出可复用的离线协议GAUGE，在τ²-bench与SimulatorArena两个基准上，对来自六家提供商的25个代理，将门控排名与带真值可验证奖励进行对比；并区分排名效度与构念效度，通过盲审小组及五类评分人群对满意度与任务成功的关系进行检验。

Result: （1）满意度-成功鸿沟：满意度几乎不携带任务成功信息，盲审评为"满意"的对话中57.5%实际未完成客户任务，该模式在五类评分人群、两个基准及所有主观维度上一致；（2）门控在广泛能力跨度上排名稳健，但在实力接近的强代理间丧失分辨率，决策分歧率由宽奖励配对的<1%跃升至接近配对的31%。

Conclusion: 该评估门控虽经人类验证却存在锚定偏差（human-validated yet mis-anchored）。作者提出"先校准后信任"（calibrate-then-trust）节奏，其中无裁判的完成位（completion bit）可作为截断回归的零成本警示器。

Abstract: Comparing and selecting task-oriented LLM agents increasingly relies on a low-cost offline evaluation gate: persona-driven LLM user-simulators converse with each candidate, an LLM-as-a-judge scores the transcripts, and the higher-scoring agent is promoted. We introduce GAUGE, a reusable offline protocol that measures whether this gate's ranking matches a grounded verifiable reward across 25 agents from six providers on the $τ^2$-bench and SimulatorArena benchmarks, separating two kinds of evaluation validity that release practices conflate: ranking validity and construct validity. First, a satisfaction-success gap: satisfaction carries essentially no information about task success, as conversations rated satisfied by our blind panel are decorrelated from actual success, with 57.5% of them failing the customer's task, a pattern consistent across five rater populations, both benchmarks, and every subjective dimension we rated. Second, while the gate's ranking is robust across the broad capability span, it loses resolution among the near-equal strong agents: this decision-disagreement rate jumps from $<$1% on wide-reward pairs to 31% on close pairs. The gate is thus human-validated yet mis-anchored. As a remedy, we propose a calibrate-then-trust cadence in which a judge-free completion bit is a zero-cost tripwire for truncation regressions.

</details>


### [80] [Repair Before Reinforce: Context-Augmented Knowledge Graph Reasoning for Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12230)
*Tharaka D. Fonseka,Niraj K. Jha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种上下文增强的多跳问答训练框架：为每个KG主三元组附加来自同一文本块的支撑三元组构成上下文图，形成KG-grounded与CG-grounded两种监督设置，并结合LLM判定的自适应修复流水线与强化学习，在胃轻瘫和糖尿病KG上验证，上下文增强监督在多跳任务上稳定优于仅KG监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答需要在多个关联事实上进行推理，而知识图谱可结构化表示这些事实。但仅用孤立的头-关系-尾三元组训练大语言模型，可能限制其学习多跳推理所需的上下文信息。

Method: 基于GraphMERT提取疾病特异KG（胃轻瘫、糖尿病）；为每个主三元组附加同一文本块的支撑三元组构建上下文图（CG），形成KG-grounded与CG-grounded两种监督设置；用SFT训练Qwen3-14B得到KGModel与CGModel；引入LLM判定的、历史感知的自适应修复流水线，识别未解决的一跳失败、持续微调修复样本并剔除或隔离噪声三元组；最后用低跳问答数据进行强化学习，并在3/4/5跳任务上评估泛化。

Result: 修复阶段使模型在清洗后保留的一跳验证集上达到100%准确率；在两种疾病上，上下文增强监督相比仅KG监督持续提升多跳性能；从修复后的SFT检查点初始化的RL带来更大且更稳定的增益。

Conclusion: 上下文增强监督能有效改善多跳问答能力，低跳修复与RL初始化策略可进一步提升并稳定多跳泛化性能。

Abstract: Question-answering often requires reasoning across multiple connected facts rather than retrieving a single isolated relation. Knowledge graphs (KGs) provide a structured way to represent such facts, but training large language models (LLMs) only on isolated KG head-relation-tail triples may limit their ability to learn the surrounding context needed for multi-hop reasoning. In this work, we propose a context-augmented training framework for multi-hop question-answering. Although generally applicable, we validate the framework in the context of disease-specific KGs, extracted using a reliable KG extraction framework called GraphMERT, for Gastroparesis and Diabetes. For each primary KG triple, we attach supporting triples extracted from the same source text chunk to form a context graph (CG). This creates two supervision settings: KG-grounded supervision, which uses only the target KG triple or path, and CG-grounded supervision, which uses the target KG triple or path together with supporting context triples. We train the Qwen3-14B model using supervised fine-tuning (SFT) under both settings, producing KGModel and CGModel variants. To strengthen the lower-hop factual foundation of the models, we introduce an LLM-judged, history-aware adaptive repair pipeline that identifies unresolved one-hop failures, continually fine-tunes on targeted repair examples, and removes or quarantines problematic noisy triples. This repair stage enables the models to reach 100% accuracy on the cleaned retained one-hop validation sets. Finally, we employ reinforcement learning (RL) using lower-hop question-answer items and evaluate generalization on harder 3-hop, 4-hop, and 5-hop tasks. Across both diseases, context-augmented supervision consistently improves multi-hop performance over KG-only supervision. RL initialized from repaired SFT checkpoints yields larger and more stable gains.

</details>


### [81] [Chopthin-Consensus Power Sampling: A Diversity-Preserving Approach to LLM Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12243)
*Minoo Ahmadi,Seyedarmin Azizi,Erfan Baghaei Potraghloo,Mehdi Kamal,Massoud Pedram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 CCPS，用 Chopthin 重采样替换等权重采样以保留推理路径多样性，并结合语义多数选择，在三个模型五个基准上提升推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 SMC 的推理时 Power Sampling 多采用等权重采样，会激进剪除低权重轨迹，丢弃可能正确的推理路径，降低搜索空间的谱系多样性。

Method: 将 Chopthin 重采样器用于 LLM 解码：不对权重做等化与冗余粒子复制，而是对最大与最小权重之比施加上界并保留不等权重；该干预保持加权 SMC 近似的条件期望不变，并保证重采样后 ESS 的下界。随后用语义多数选择：合并 token 完全相同的最终轨迹，对语义等价答案聚类，返回被最多不同轨迹支持的答案。

Result: 在三个开源权重模型与五个推理基准上，Chopthin 在 15 个设置中的 13 个提升 oracle 覆盖率；结合语义多数选择后，CCPS 在 15 个设置中的 14 个匹配或超过 Power-SMC 基线的最终答案准确率，绝对提升最高达 10.6 个百分点。

Conclusion: 保多样性重采样与多样性感知选择是互补机制，可共同提升免训练 LLM 推理性能。代码已开源。

Abstract: Inference-time power sampling via Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) can substantially improve large language model (LLM) reasoning without requiring post-training. However, many existing SMC approaches rely on equal-weight resampling, which can aggressively prune low-weight trajectories, discarding potentially correct reasoning paths and degrading the genealogical diversity of the search space. To address this, we introduce Chopthin-Consensus Power Sampling (CCPS). Our method applies the Chopthin resampler to LLM decoding: rather than equalizing weights and forcing unnecessary particle duplication, it enforces an upper bound on the ratio between the largest and smallest weights and carries the unequal weights forward. This targeted intervention preserves a richer set of distinct reasoning paths, keeps the weighted SMC approximation unchanged in conditional expectation, and guarantees a lower bound on the post-resampling effective sample size (ESS). To fully exploit this enriched population, we employ a semantic-majority selection mechanism that merges token-identical final trajectories, clusters semantically equivalent answers, and returns the answer supported by the largest number of distinct trajectories. Evaluating across three open-weight models and five reasoning benchmarks, we show that Chopthin increases oracle coverage in 13 of 15 settings. Combined with semantic-majority selection, CCPS matches or exceeds the final-answer accuracy of the Power-SMC baseline in 14 of 15 settings, delivering absolute gains of up to 10.6 percentage points. These findings demonstrate that diversity-preserving resampling and diversity-aware selection are complementary mechanisms for training-free LLM reasoning. Code is available at github.com/MinooAhmadii/chopthin-consensus-power-sampling.

</details>


### [82] [Automated Detection and Structuring of Social Tipping Point Evidence in Climate related Documents: A Modular AI Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12254)
*Kavindu Perera,Mohammad Abaeiani,Ekaterina Gilman,Lauri Loven,Mourad Oussalah,Tassos Kanellos,Beatrice Gobbo,Dante Adami,Nicolò Ferriani,Maximiliano Romero,Pierre Rossel,Marc Bonazountas,Christina Deligianni,Nikos Xyderis,Artur Bogucki,Lampros Argyriou,Prasasthy Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模块化的基于Transformer的开源框架，在段落级别检测并结构化气候文献中的社会临界点证据，整合分段、检测、改写、评级与语义检索五个组件，并通过基准测试验证其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候文献增长速度快于评审团队的阅读能力，而"环境社会临界点"这类概念的证据往往仅存在于长文档的一两段中。现有文本挖掘工具只能按主题分类整篇文档或标注孤立论断，无法系统性地发现和组织这类关键证据，导致大量重要证据缺乏系统化的挖掘与整理方法。

Method: 提出一个开源、模块化的Transformer框架，在段落级别检测与结构化社会临界点证据，将五个组件集成为单一可部署工作流：DistilBERT边界分割器（分段）、迭代增强的RoBERTa分类器（检测）、Mistral 7B（改写检测到的段落以提升清晰度）、LLaMA 3.2 3B（依据五条已发表的社会临界点标准对段落评分）、Milvus向量存储（语义检索）。系统封装于Streamlit界面，后端使用MinIO对象存储。

Result: 在GPT-4.1标注的163段落基准和专家评审的51段落集合上评估：分割器在九项指标综合得分（6.137）上超越三种对比方法；调优后的RoBERTa在完整基准上准确率71.4%、Cohen's kappa为0.337，在带标签段落上准确率87.5%、kappa为0.742，优于气候领域专用模型和未调优的语言模型。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个可部署、开源的段落级框架，能够系统性地发现与组织气候文献中分散的社会临界点证据，弥补了现有整篇分类与孤立论断挖掘工具的不足，并通过基准实验验证了各组件方法的有效性。

Abstract: The climate literature has grown faster than review teams can read it. That gap matters most for a concept like the environmental social tipping point, the threshold at which a small change triggers rapid, self-reinforcing change in a social system. Evidence of this kind of shift is usually contained in one or two paragraphs within a longer document. As a result, existing text mining tools-which categorize entire documents by topic or highlight isolated claims-leave an expanding set of important evidence without any systematic method for discovery or organization. This paper presents an open and modular transformer-based framework that detects and structures social tipping point evidence at the passage level. The framework joins five components into a single deployable workflow: a DistilBERT boundary splitter for segmentation, an iteratively augmented RoBERTa classifier for detection, a Mistral 7B model that rewrites each detected passage for clarity, a LLaMA 3.2 3B model that rates the passage against five published social tipping point criteria, and a Milvus vector store for semantic retrieval. The system is wrapped in a Streamlit interface backed by MinIO object storage. Evaluated on a 163-passage benchmark labelled by GPT-4.1 and a 51-passage set reviewed by experts, the splitter surpassed three competing methods on a nine-metric composite score (6.137). The tuned RoBERTa model achieved 71.4 percent accuracy with a Cohen's kappa of 0.337 on the full benchmark, and 87.5 percent accuracy with a kappa of 0.742 on passages with labels, outperforming both a climate-focused model and untuned language models.

</details>


### [83] [HypoKG: Evidence-Disciplined Biomedical Hypothesis Generation Beyond Endpoint Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12260)
*Dominic Okonkwo,Adetayo Okunoye,Ismailcem Budak Arpinar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究将KEGG、Rhea与UniProt整合为统一生化知识图谱，构建550条“酶来源—罕见病终点”路径基准，用6个LLM在4种信息条件下生成13,200条假设并以五准则1-5分专家评分法评价，发现仅给端点时假设得分高但证据支撑弱，给完整生物路径时推理更符合已知机制，即“证据约束推理”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能生成生物医学假设，但其究竟是真正基于科学证据推理，还是仅生成看似可信的观点，尚不清楚。为此需要一个可控评估框架，区分信息量与证据结构对假设质量与证据支撑度的影响。

Method: 整合KEGG、Rhea与UniProt为统一生化知识图谱，构建550条连接酶来源与罕见病终点的路径基准；6个LLM在4种生物信息条件下（仅来源酶、完整生物路径、仅来源与疾病终点等）生成13,200条假设；采用专家制定的五准则、每准则1-5分评分表打分；并做路径中间步骤打乱、端点固定的扰动实验以检验模型是否真正利用路径结构。

Result: 同时获得来源与疾病端点的模型常产出评分最高的假设，说明LLM能从极少信息生成引人注目的想法，但这类假设证据根基较弱；获得完整生物路径的模型生成的假设更符合已知机制关系。打乱中间路径步骤后，证据支撑度显著下降（delta = -0.793，p < 0.001），证实模型在推理中确实使用了路径结构。

Conclusion: 知识图谱以两种方式支持假设生成：一是识别文献中缺失的生物端点配对，二是其机制路径引导LLM在端点之间如何推理。LLM在信息稀缺时易产生“高说服力低证据”的假设，而提供机制路径可诱发证据约束推理。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate biomedical hypotheses, but it remains unclear whether they truly reason from scientific evidence or simply produce convincing-sounding ideas. To study this, we combine three major biological databases: the Kyoto Encyclopedia of Genes and Genomes (KEGG), Rhea, and UniProt, into a unified biochemical knowledge graph and construct a benchmark of 550 paths connecting enzyme sources to rare disease endpoints, yielding 13,200 hypotheses from six LLMs under four conditions varying the biological information each model receives: source enzyme only, full biological path, or source and disease endpoint only. Hypotheses are scored using an expert-derived five-criterion rubric on a 1-5 scale per criterion. We find that models given both the source and disease endpoint often produce the highest-scoring hypotheses, showing that LLMs can generate compelling ideas from minimal information. However, these hypotheses are less grounded in the evidence. In contrast, models given the full biological path generate hypotheses more consistent with known mechanistic relationships. We call this evidence-disciplined reasoning. To confirm this effect, we shuffled intermediate path steps while keeping endpoints fixed. Evidence grounding dropped significantly (delta = -0.793, p < 0.001), confirming models genuinely used path structure during reasoning. Our findings show that knowledge graphs support hypothesis generation in two ways: they identify biological endpoint pairs absent from the literature, and their mechanistic paths guide how LLMs reason between them.

</details>


### [84] [EAR: Entity-Aware Partitioning Approach for Retrieval-Augmented Generation Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12268)
*Cenab Batu Bora,Oylum Alatlı,Sebnem Bora,Oguz Dikenelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出EAR（实体感知分区）方法，用问句、选项与语料中的规范化实体锚点构建检索单元，替代固定长度分块；在MMLU风格153题上，实体窗口比传统分块减少37.5–40.2%的检索词量，但准确率变化不显著，贡献主要为方法性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG可提升知识密集型问答，但"如何将源语料切分为可检索单元"这一首要设计选择常被忽视；固定大小分块易返回与问题仅存在隐含关系的长段落，检索单元与问题相关性不直接。

Method: 提出EAR（Entity-Aware Partitioning）：从问题、答案选项和语料中抽取规范化表面锚点；检索与语料锚点匹配的局部窗口；并可通过抽取式摘要挂接更大的父段落。在经自动语料支持启发式筛选的153道MMLU风格题目上评估，使用去污染公开教科书文本，以Mistral、Gemma、DeepSeek模型进行同协议top-k=3与top-k=8的扫描对比。

Result: 实体窗口相对分块减少37.5–40.2%的检索词量。观察到的准确率变化：top-k=3时分别为+5.2、+1.3、-3.9个百分点；top-k=8时分别为+5.9、-3.3、-4.6个百分点；所有实体窗口差异均不具统计显著性。

Conclusion: EAR提供了紧凑且可检视的检索单元，其限定范围内的贡献为方法性；基于规则的锚点抽取器仍具领域特异性，迁移前需单独验证。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can improve knowledge-intensive question answering, but the first design choice is easy to overlook: how should the source corpus be partitioned into retrievable units? Fixed-size chunks often return long passages whose relation to the question is only implicit. We introduce EAR, an Entity-Aware Partitioning approach for multiple-choice question answering (MCQA). EAR extracts normalized surface anchors from the question, answer options, and corpus; retrieves local windows around matching corpus anchors; and can attach a larger parent passage through an extractive summary. We evaluate EAR on a cleaned Massive Multitask Language Understanding (MMLU)-style subset of 153 questions selected by an automatic corpus-support heuristic and using decontaminated public textbook text. Across same-protocol top-k = 3 and top-k = 8 sweeps with Mistral, Gemma, and DeepSeek, EAR entity-window reduces retrieved words by 37.5-40.2% relative to chunks. Observed accuracy changes are +5.2, +1.3, and -3.9 points at top-k = 3, and +5.9, -3.3, and -4.6 points at top-k = 8; none of the entity-window differences is statistically significant. The scoped contribution is methodological: EAR provides a compact and inspectable retrieval unit, while its rule-based anchor extractor remains domain-specific and requires separate validation before transfer.

</details>


### [85] [Breaking the Token Ceiling: Distilling Smaller, Stronger Byte Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12303)
*Kalyani Marathe,Artidoro Pagnoni,Tomasz Limisiewicz,Margaret Li,Mike Lewis,Luke Zettlemoyer,Srinivasan Iyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文首次大规模对比了蒸馏与交叉熵目标下、Token与Byte两种分词方案的解码器模型缩放趋势。作者提出Marginalize-It（近似）和End-Of-Token（精确）两种将token logits转为byte logits的方法，训练约1B参数、最多1万亿字节数据的模型。结果表明：Byte模型在低FLOP时弱于Token模型，但随算力增加反超，蒸馏End-Of-Token-1B渐近超越蒸馏Token-1B最多4%，且数据效率高六倍，logit存储成本降至约五分之一，预测将超越Llama 3.2-1B等模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小模型通过从共享分词方案的大模型蒸馏获得能力提升，但蒸馏后的byte模型与token模型在算力与数据增加时的缩放趋势是否一致尚不清楚，缺乏系统性的对比研究。

Method: 引入两种将token logits高效转换为byte logits的变体：近似的Marginalize-It与精确的End-Of-Token。开展首次大规模研究，在约1B参数的层-参数匹配解码器密集型Transformer上，同时变化分词方案（Tokens、Bytes、Bytes w/ eot）与训练目标（蒸馏vs.交叉熵），扫描至约1万亿字节数据，覆盖八个基准（三类任务）。

Result: 在低FLOP区间，Token-1B模型优于byte模型（End-Of-Token-1B与Bytes-1B），但最终趋于平台；byte模型起步较差却随算力增加反超Token-1B，达到更高的下游任务性能上限。外推平均top-1错误率对验证BPB的缩放律，渐近上蒸馏End-Of-Token-1B比蒸馏Token-1B优最多4%；其数据效率更高，仅用六分之一训练数据即可匹配蒸馏Token-1B。此外，通过256字节小词表规避了logit dump时的top-k截断需求，logit存储成本降至约五分之一。缩放律预测蒸馏End-Of-Token-1B渐近上平均下游任务超越Llama 3.2-1B、Gemma-3-1B-pt和Gemma 2B分别最多6.5%、8.1%和2.1%。

Conclusion: Byte级模型（尤其蒸馏End-Of-Token）在更大算力和数据下展现出优于Token模型的缩放趋势与性能上限，并具备更高数据效率、更低logit存储成本及规避top-k截断的优势，为蒸馏分词方案选择提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Small models are made more capable through distillation from a larger one that shares their tokenization scheme. However, do distilled byte and token models behave similarly in terms of scaling trends as compute and data increases? To enable this comparison, we introduce two variants to efficiently convert token logits to Byte Logits: 1) approximate: Marginalize-It, and 2) exact: End-Of-Token. We then present the first large scale study of overtraining decoder-only dense transformer models varying two dimensions simultaneously: the tokenization scheme (Tokens, Bytes, Bytes w/ eot) and the training objective (Distillation vs. Cross-Entropy), sweeping layer-parameter-matched models with roughly 1 billion parameters up to 1 trillion bytes of data. Across eight benchmarks spanning three categories: Multiple Choice QA, Language Generation, and Machine Translation, we find that Token-1B models outperform byte models (End-Of-Token-1B and Bytes-1B) in the low-FLOP regime but eventually plateau; byte models start worse yet surpass Token-1B models with more compute, reaching a higher downstream task performance ceiling. Extrapolating the average top-1 error vs. validation BPB scaling laws predicts that, asymptotically, distilled End-Of-Token-1B outperforms distilled Token-1B by up to 4%. They are also far more data efficient, matching the performance of distilled Token-1B using only one-sixth of the training data. Moreover, by operating over a small vocabulary of 256 bytes instead of on the order of 100K tokens, they circumvent the need for top-k truncation during logit dumping, while also reducing logit storage costs to roughly one-fifth. Finally, our downstream performance scaling laws predict that our distilled End-Of-Token-1B models asymptotically surpass the Llama 3.2-1B, Gemma-3-1B-pt, and Gemma 2B models on averaged downstream tasks by up to 6.5%, 8.1%, and 2.1%, respectively.

</details>


### [86] [ESTS at WMT26: Routing-Informed Expert Pruning for Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12310)
*Liu O. Martin,Lucas Bandarkar,Nanyun Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了 ESTS 团队参加 WMT26 无约束模型压缩共享任务的六个提交系统，涵盖英译简中与英译埃及阿拉伯语两个方向，均基于 GPT-OSS-20B 构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受限资源下实现大模型翻译系统的高效压缩与部署，需要针对翻译任务的专家重要性评估与跨语言容量分配策略，以在显著减小模型规模的同时保持翻译质量。

Method: 采用任务特定路由质量对专家排序，并利用跨语言路由散度在层间分配保留容量，物理移除低重要性专家；随后在 GPT-5.1 生成的合成翻译数据上对专家模型进行恢复微调，并对保留的专家投影权重应用 MXFP4 量化。此外构建了面向指令条件设定的鲁棒推理系统，包含类别推断、输出验证、重试、分段回退及源端 JSON 重建。

Result: 六个提交系统的参数量介于 4.186B 至 7.770B，打包产物大小为 4.55 至 6.33 GiB；使用 GPT-5.1 伪参考的内部 xCOMET-XL 评估提供了各压缩工作点之间的内部比较。

Conclusion: 该工作通过专家剪枝、恢复微调与 MXFP4 量化相结合，实现了从 20B 到 4–8B 量级的跨语言翻译专家模型压缩，并配套鲁棒推理流程，为 WMT26 模型压缩任务提供了多档压缩工作点的系统方案。

Abstract: We describe six submissions under the team name ESTS to the unconstrained WMT26 Model Compression Shared Task for English--Simplified Chinese and English--Egyptian Arabic. We submit three compression operating points per translation direction, all derived from GPT-OSS-20B. We use task-specific routing mass to rank experts and cross-lingual routing divergence to allocate retained capacity across layers, then physically remove low-importance experts. The resulting specialists are recovery-tuned on GPT-5.1-generated synthetic translation data and further compressed by applying MXFP4 quantization to the retained expert projection weights. We additionally implement a robust inference system for the instruction-conditioned WMT26 setting, including category inference, output validation, retries, segmented fallback, and source-owned JSON reconstruction. Across our six submissions, parameter counts range from 4.186B to 7.770B and packed artifact sizes from 4.55 to 6.33~GiB. Internal xCOMET-XL evaluation using GPT-5.1 pseudo-references provides an internal comparison across the submitted compression operating points.

</details>


### [87] [I Am No One: Style-Aware Paraphrasing for Text Anonymization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12341)
*Ahmed Sohair Khan,Estrid He,Monica Wachowicz,Elham Naghizade*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种风格感知的提示驱动匿名化方法，利用大语言模型构建紧凑风格画像并改写文本，在保持语义与可读性的同时将作者归属F1降低60-70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者归属模型可借助稳定的文体指纹，从看似匿名的文本中重新识别用户，即使用户显式标识已被移除，对文本发布和分析构成隐私风险。该风险同样存在于语音衍生文本（如会议与呼叫中心的ASR转写）中，即便经过声学匿名化，文体泄漏依旧存在。而基于差分隐私的匿名化往往严重损害文本质量与可用性。

Method: 提出一种风格感知、提示驱动的匿名化方法：使用预训练大语言模型，从极少量样本中构建紧凑的风格画像，并据此改写文本，以抑制可识别的风格标记，同时保留原意。

Result: 在博客与评论数据集上，该方法将作者归属F1降低60-70%，同时维持内容质量与可读性，显著优于基于差分隐私及非差分隐私的基线方法。

Conclusion: 风格感知的提示驱动改写能够在语义与可读性基本不受损的前提下有效对抗文体指纹识别，为文本匿名化提供了一种优于DP方案的新思路。

Abstract: Authorship attribution models can re-identify users from seemingly anonymized text by exploiting stable stylistic fingerprints, even after explicit identifiers are removed, posing a growing privacy risk for text publishing and analytics. This risk extends to speech-derived text such as ASR transcripts of meetings and call-center conversations, where stylometric leakage can persist even after acoustic anonymization. Differential privacy-based anonymization often severely degrades text quality and utility. We propose a style-aware, prompt-driven anonymization approach that uses pretrained large language models to construct compact stylistic profiles from minimal samples and rewrite text to suppress identifiable style markers while preserving meaning. Across blog and review datasets, our approach reduces authorship attribution F1 by 60-70% while maintaining content quality and readability, substantially outperforming DP-based and non-DP baselines.

</details>


### [88] [SynthSentry: Detecting Synthetic Data Contamination in Language Model Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12353)
*Praveen Kumar Myakala,Ravichandra Namburi,Sowmya Keragodu Jayaramu,Sooraj George Thomas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 SynthSentry，一种语料级、与生成模型无关的合成数据污染检测信号，用于训练前筛查未知来源语料，并在多生成器留一协议和领域分层假阳性研究中验证其排序与校准能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归训练导致模型坍塌（分布尾部与事实准确性下降而流畅性保留），但已有工作多为训练后的诊断；真正可操作的问题是在训练前筛查来源未知的语料。现有方法往往依赖生成模型、生成历史或合成标签，实用性受限，因此需要一个无需这些条件的语料级污染信号。

Method: SynthSentry 为语料级、模型无关的污染评分，不需要访问生成模型、生成历史或合成标签。评分基于三项统计量的分布散度：词汇多样性坍塌、n-gram 尾部截断、跨参考模型的困惑度方差。评估采用小规模开放权重生成器及一个指令微调开放权重模型污染语料，并用留一生成器协议；同时进行领域分层研究，在自然重复性人类文本（法律、临床、源代码）上测量假阳性。校准上以协方差收缩和 bootstrap 阈值替代朴素分位数。

Result: 该评分能在整个生成器家族被留出时仍较好地按严重程度对语料排序，损失很小。按领域校准时假阳性接近名义预算，而朴素分位数方法的假阳性约为预算的四倍。下游微调检查未发现该规模下由污染导致的准确率损失，因此剪枝能否恢复性能仍待验证；同一次运行显示当剪枝比例超过真实污染比例时存在过度剪枝风险。

Conclusion: 本文将筛查定位为数据策管防护而非事后诊断，并发布评分工具包。局限在于结果均为小规模、仅限英语、批处理模式语料筛查；污染源为单代生成或人工撰写而非递归生成，因此结论适用于一般合成污染，不能外推到递归深度。

Abstract: Large language models trained recursively on their own or other models' outputs undergo model collapse, in which distributional tails and factual accuracy deteriorate while fluency survives. Prior work diagnoses collapse after training; the actionable problem is screening a corpus of unknown provenance before training. We introduce SynthSentry, a corpus-level, model-agnostic contamination signal requiring no access to the generating model, no generation history, and no synthetic labels. The score is a distributional divergence over three statistics: lexical diversity collapse, n-gram tail truncation, and perplexity variance across reference models. We evaluate on corpora contaminated by small open-weight generators and an instruction-tuned open-weight model under a leave-one-generator-out protocol. A domain-stratified study measures false positives on naturally repetitive human text (legal, clinical, source code). The score ranks corpora by severity with little loss when whole generator families are held out. Per-domain calibration holds near its nominal false-positive budget once covariance shrinkage and a bootstrap threshold replace a naive quantile, which runs four times over budget. A downstream fine-tuning check showed no contamination-driven accuracy deficit at our scale, so whether pruning recovers one remains open; the same run shows over-pruning risk once pruning exceeds the true contamination fraction. We frame screening as a data-curation defense rather than a post-hoc diagnosis and release the scoring toolkit. All results are small-scale; scope is English-language, batch-mode corpus screening. Contamination sources are single-generation or hand-authored rather than recursively generated, so results speak to synthetic contamination generally and not to recursion depth.

</details>


### [89] [CueMem: Cue-Guided Context Reconstruction for Long-Term Conversational Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12354)
*Changjian Wang,Rongzhen Li,Weili Guan,Shuming Shi,Quan Lu,Ning Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CueMem：将记忆记录视为检索线索而非自足证据，通过线索引导从源对话轮重建查询相关上下文，在LoCoMo和LongMemEval上超越长时记忆基线，同时减少输入token和延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期对话智能体需从冗长对话历史中回忆信息来回答查询。直接用完整历史成本高且常不可靠，而压缩后的记忆单元可能丢失问答所需的细粒度证据。受自传体记忆的"重构观"启发，作者希望找到一种介于二者之间的方案。

Method: CueMem框架：记忆构建阶段，从对话轮中抽取细粒度记忆线索，并将每条线索链接到其源对话轮；查询阶段，检索与查询相关的线索，将其映射到源轮锚点，并沿捕捉时间邻近性与语义相关性的对话轮图从锚点扩展，从而从原始对话中重建紧凑的证据上下文，供LLM生成答案。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval上，CueMem持续优于具代表性的长期记忆基线。进一步分析显示，基于图的上下文重建有助于恢复支撑性对话证据，同时相比使用完整历史输入的LLM设置，降低了查询时的输入token数量与延迟。

Conclusion: 检索线索可作为自足记忆证据的有效替代方案，用于长期对话问答。

Abstract: Long-term conversational agents must answer user queries by recalling information from extended dialogue histories, yet directly using the full history is costly and often unreliable, while compressed memory units may lose fine-grained evidence needed for question answering. Motivated by the reconstructive view of autobiographical memory, we propose CueMem, a cue-guided framework that treats extracted memory records as retrieval cues rather than self-contained evidence and reconstructs query-relevant dialogue context from their source turns. During memory construction, CueMem extracts fine-grained memory cues from dialogue turns and links each cue to its source turn. At query time, it retrieves query-relevant cues, maps them to source-turn anchors, and expands from these anchors over a turn graph that captures temporal proximity and semantic relatedness, reconstructing a compact evidence context from the original dialogue for LLM answer generation. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval show that CueMem consistently outperforms representative long-term memory baselines. Further analyses show that graph-based context reconstruction helps recover supporting dialogue evidence while reducing query-time input tokens and latency compared with the full-history LLM setting. These results highlight retrieval cues as an effective alternative to self-contained memory evidence for long-term conversational question answering.

</details>


### [90] [ORQA: An Occupation-Realistic Question and Answer Framework for LLM Professional Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12366)
*Shreyas Krishnan,Serina Chang,Abhishek Nagaraj*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ORQA通过将O*NET职业与可信的职业专属网站（监管机构、许可机构、专业组织、政府出版物）相连接，自动与人工审核结合生成可溯源问答对，构建覆盖116个职业、480道题的职业级知识基准，测试15个前沿与开源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估职业级LLM知识的方法存在局限：或通过任务定义将抽象LLM技能映射到职业，或依赖难以大规模获取且成本高昂的专家知识。作者希望提供一种可扩展、低成本且可溯源的替代方案，以评估AI在专业领域的职业级表现。

Method: ORQA将O*NET职业与可信的职业专属网站（监管机构、许可机构、专业组织、政府出版物）关联，通过自动化流水线加人工审核，转化为可溯源的职业相关问答题。题目集覆盖SOC全部21个大类的116个职业，480道题源自187个网站，每题均针对该职业的真实世界技能问题。随后用该基准测试15个前沿与开源模型。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6、GPT-5.4和Claude Sonnet 4.6表现最佳，约58–62%；较小的开源模型约33–41%。跨职业差异显著：医疗相关职业最高（78%），办公与行政支持约40%，个别职业（如钣金工、渔猎监督员）几乎为零。开放式问题与按工资总额加权均未显著影响模型排名。

Conclusion: 利用现有可信的职业专属信息来测试LLM在专业领域的知识，可能是一种可扩展且有价值的职业级AI性能评估方法。结果与数据见orqabench.org。

Abstract: We present ORQA, a method for testing occupation-level knowledge in large language models. Prior methods either map abstract LLM skills to occupations via task definitions or utilize expert knowledge which is difficult to obtain at scale and expensive. ORQA complements both of these methods by connecting O*NET occupations to trusted occupation-specific websites (such as regulatory agencies, licensing bodies, professional organizations, and government publications) and converting these into source-traceable question-answer pairs. A combination of an automated pipeline and human review produces a set of high quality questions about occupations. The question set created via our method covers 116 occupations from all 21 major groups in the SOC, with 480 questions sourced from 187 different websites. Each question is designed to probe a real-world skill question that is relevant to the occupation in question. We test 15 state-of-the-art frontier and open-weight models via this method. Claude Opus 4.6, GPT-5.4 and Claude Sonnet 4.6 all perform the best at approximately 58-62% while smaller open-weight models achieve approximately 33-41% performance. Performance varies significantly across occupations. Healthcare-related occupations achieve the highest performance (78%) while Office and Administrative Support achieve approximately 40%. Performance on individual occupations (e.g. Sheet Metal Workers and Fish and Game Wardens) is essentially zero. We also find that open-ended questions and weighting by wage bill do not significantly affect the ranking of models on this benchmark. We believe that leveraging existing trusted occupation-specific information to test LLM knowledge in professional domains may be a scalable and useful method for evaluating occupation-level AI performance in the future. Results and data are available at orqabench.org.

</details>


### [91] [Representation-based Masked Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12382)
*Yangrong Hu,Ding Huang,Xueyu Zhou,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RMDM框架，利用文本表示编码全局语义，指导掩码扩散模型的并行token更新，提升生成质量尤其在少步采样场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有掩码扩散模型的并行采样独立更新多个掩码token，忽略掩码token间的相互依赖，缺乏全局协调，导致输出不连贯。

Method: 先用预训练编码器将文本编码到连续语义空间，学习可逆变换将表示分布归一化为高斯先验以支持高效采样；再以该潜在语义表示为条件训练掩码扩散模型学习条件文本分布，使表示作为全局语义引导协调并行token更新。

Result: 实验表明RMDM显著提升生成质量，尤其在激进的少步采样情形下表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过文本表示显式编码全局语义，RMDM有效改善了并行采样的协调性与生成质量，为掩码扩散语言模型提供了新的建模思路。

Abstract: Masked Diffusion Models (MDMs) have emerged as a compelling paradigm for language modeling, offering the capability for efficient parallel text generation. However, existing parallel sampling methods typically update multiple masked tokens independently and ignore the complex mutual dependencies among the masked tokens. This independent updating mechanism lacks global coordination and might lead to incoherent outputs. To address this limitation, we propose Representation-based Masked Diffusion Model (RMDM), a framework that leverages the text representation to explicitly encode global semantics and help to parallel update tokens more precisely. Specifically, we first encode text into a continuous semantic space using a pretrained encoder and learn an invertible transformation that normalizes the representation distribution to a Gaussian prior, facilitating efficient sampling during generation. Conditioned on this latent semantic representation, we train a masked diffusion model to learn the conditional text distribution, where the representation serves as global semantic guidance to coordinate parallel token updates and faithfully approximate the target distribution. Empirical results demonstrate that RMDM significantly improves generation quality, particularly in aggressive few-step sampling regimes.

</details>


### [92] [GraphProfiler: Source-Linked Sensitive Attribute Inference via Personal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12448)
*Ahmed Sohair Khan,Estrid He,Chenglong Ma,Monica Wachowicz,Elham Naghizade*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GraphProfiler：将用户发帖历史构建为可追溯来源的个人知识图谱，实现可审计的LLM属性推断，攻击成功率接近纯文本基线，且为98%以上预测引用证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 敏感属性（年龄、收入、职业等）可通过聚合大量普通帖子中的间接线索被推断出来。基于LLM的画像器能自动高精度完成这种聚合，构成大规模隐私威胁。但现有LLM画像器无法说明是哪些帖子、概念与关系促成了推断，而这正是指向性隐私缓解（只删改真正泄露属性的少数帖子，而非扰动全部历史）的关键。

Method: 提出GraphProfiler：把每个用户的发帖历史表示为带来源链接的个人知识图谱，节点和边可追溯到原始帖子；并将属性预测解析为被引用的图记录与源文本，从而实现可审计性。

Result: 在八属性SynthPAI基准上达到86.7%攻击成功率，与强纯文本基线相差不到两点；在PANDORA上为84.6%；对98%以上的预测给出支持证据。消融实验显示，删除被引用帖子导致的攻击成功率下降显著大于删除等量随机帖子，表明被引用帖子确实促成攻击成功。

Conclusion: GraphProfiler在保持接近最先进攻击性能的同时，首次为LLM属性推断提供可追溯、可引证的审计能力，为通过精准删改少量泄露帖子来缓解隐私风险提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Sensitive attributes such as age, income, and occupation can be inferred from user-generated content by aggregating indirect cues across many ordinary posts. LLM-based profilers can perform this aggregation automatically and with high accuracy, which makes large-scale personal attribute inference a major privacy threat. Existing LLM-based profilers, however, offer limited insight into which specific posts, concepts, and relationships made an inference possible, which is key to targeted privacy mitigation, i.e., redacting or rewriting only the few posts that actually leak an attribute, rather than perturbing entire histories. We introduce GraphProfiler, an auditable LLM-based profiler that represents each user's post history as a source-linked personal knowledge graph where nodes and edges trace back to the originating post and resolves attribute predictions to cited graph records and source texts. GraphProfiler reaches 86.7% attack success rate on the eight-attribute SynthPAI benchmark, within two points of strong text-only baselines, and 84.6% on PANDORA, while citing supporting evidence for over 98% of predictions. Our controlled ablation experiments provide evidence that the cited posts contribute to attack success, as removing them reduces the attack success rate substantially more than removing an equal number of random posts.

</details>


### [93] [Not All Speech Is Intent: Adaptive Self-Correcting Inference Layer for Post-ASR False Wake-Up](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12469)
*Preeti Saraswat,Divya Neelagiri,Anil Yadav*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ASCIL——一种反馈驱动的自适应自校正推理层，作为 ASR 后的补充纠错框架，在响应生成前重新评估唤醒意图，通过融合声学嵌入、语言线索、设备上下文与历史误判模式来减少误唤醒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 误唤醒激活是对话式 AI 的持续难题：与唤醒词语音相似的语音可能生成语法合法、语义连贯的 ASR 转写，导致助手错误执行。现有系统多在孤立情况下做出单一意图决策，缺乏从重复错误中学习、通过个性化学习适应用户的机制。

Method: ASCIL 融合声学嵌入、语言线索、设备上下文以及过去误分类模式，在响应生成前重新评估唤醒意图。它将隐式信号（犹豫、脱离、沉默）与显式信号（取消、重复）视为自动推断的、带噪声的潜在误分类行为指标，据此驱动在线模式更新，无需人工标注（离线评估所用的有意/无意参考标签由人工标注）。系统可从既往错误中泛化，在推理时施加校正调整，并与自然语言执行并行持续更新。

Result: 在包含 3,667 次交互、覆盖 14 种声学与上下文条件、带人工标注有意/无意参考标签的专有数据集上评估：在由基线失败构成的会话不相交子集上实现 54.27% 的相对错误率下降；在问题标注评估切片上，阈值 0.90 时实现最高 24.39% 的相对错误率下降。同时提升有意接受率，报告基准中中位附加延迟低于 60 ms。

Conclusion: ASCIL 作为一种互补的 ASR 后校正层，能够在不依赖人工标注的在线学习条件下有效降低误唤醒导致的错误执行，且延迟开销极低，具有实际部署可行性。

Abstract: False wake-up activations remain a persistent challenge in conversational AI. Speech phonetically similar to a device's wake word can produce a syntactically valid and semantically coherent ASR transcript that the assistant incorrectly executes. Most existing systems make a single intent decision in isolation, without a mechanism to learn from recurring errors over time or adapt to individual users through personalized learning. We introduce the Feedback-Driven Adaptive Self-Correcting Inference Layer (ASCIL), a complementary post-ASR correction framework that re-evaluates wake-up intent before response generation by fusing acoustic embeddings, linguistic cues, device context, and patterns from past misclassifications. ASCIL interprets implicit signals, including hesitation, disengagement, and silence, and explicit signals, including cancellation and repetition, as automatically inferred, noisy behavioral indicators of potential misclassification. These signals drive online pattern updates without manual annotation, whereas the intentional/unintentional reference labels used for offline evaluation are human-annotated. It generalizes from prior errors, applies corrective adjustments at inference time, and continuously updates in parallel with natural-language execution. Evaluated on a proprietary dataset of 3,667 interactions with human-annotated intentional/unintentional reference labels spanning 14 acoustic and contextual conditions, ASCIL achieves 54.27% relative error reduction on a session-disjoint subset constructed from baseline failures, and up to 24.39% relative error reduction at threshold 0.90 on the issue-tagged evaluation slice. These gains are achieved while improving intentional acceptance rates, with a median added latency below 60 ms in the reported benchmark.

</details>


### [94] [AMDKernelVault: Large-Scale Datasets and Agentic Training for AMD GPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12471)
*Ji Liu,Saptarshi Majumder,Yiqing Huang,Wenwen Ouyang,Umang Pandey,Zeping Li,Chushi Chen,Zihao An,Puyuan Yang,Zekai Li,Sina Rafati,Ziqiong Liu,Pratik Prabhanjan Brahma,Dong Li,Zicheng Liu,Sharon Zhou,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 AMDKernelVault，一个面向 AMD CDNA GPU 的开放 HIP 与 Triton 内核语料库及训练框架，并训练 Qwen3-8B 展示其可用性，在多个基准上取得最高正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的内核智能体以 CUDA/NVIDIA 为中心，且常依赖反复调用前沿 LLM 进行生成、反思与优化，缺少面向 AMD 平台的数据与工具支持。为弥补这一空白，作者构建了面向 AMD CDNA GPU 的开放内核语料库与训练框架。

Method: 开发 HIPKernelGen 与 TritonKernelGen 两条由智能体驱动的流水线，将 PyTorch 参考实现转换为 HIP 或 Triton 内核，在 ROCm 下编译和验证候选内核，并在 AMD 硬件上进行延迟分析。语料库包含 62,153 条经执行验证的 HIP 内核样本、2,377 条基于生产的 ROCm Libraries QA 条目和 39,893 个 Triton 内核。随后对 Qwen3-8B 进行监督微调与执行感知强化学习。

Result: 在固定评估预算下，所训练模型在 PyTorch-to-HIP 上 Pass@1 达 34.0%，TritonBench-G 上 Corr@3 达 33.2%，ROCmBench 上 Corr@3 达 41.94%，在所比较模型中正确率最高；但在编译与速度指标上并非全面领先。

Conclusion: AMDKernelVault 为 AMD 平台提供了可复用的内核语料库与训练框架，验证了其用于训练内核生成模型的实用价值。语料库与文档发布于 Hugging Face，相关训练与内核生成代码发布于 GitHub。

Abstract: We introduce AMDKernelVault, an open HIP and Triton kernel corpus and training framework for recent AMD CDNA GPUs. Existing LLM-based kernel agents are largely CUDA/NVIDIA-centric and often depend on repeated frontier-LLM calls for generation, reflection, and optimization. To address this gap, we develop HIPKernelGen and TritonKernelGen, agent-driven pipelines that transform PyTorch references into HIP or Triton kernels, compile and validate candidates under ROCm, and latency-profile them on AMD hardware. The corpus contains 62,153 execution-verified HIP kernel samples, 2,377 production-grounded ROCm Libraries QA entries, and 39,893 Triton kernels. We further train Qwen3-8B with supervised fine-tuning and execution-aware reinforcement learning as a demonstration of the corpus's utility. Under fixed evaluation budgets, it achieves the highest correctness among the compared models on PyTorch-to-HIP (34.0% Pass@1), TritonBench-G (33.2% Corr@3), and ROCmBench (41.94% Corr@3), but does not uniformly lead compilation or speed metrics. The corpus and documentation are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/amd/AIG-Datasets, and the associated training and kernel-generation code is available at https://github.com/AMD-AGI/hip_kernel_llm_lab.

</details>


### [95] [Zipbench: Low-Cost Framework for Compressing Comprehensive Benchmarks of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12475)
*Zhongzhan Huang,Junxin Li,Guoming Ling,Yupei Lin,Shanshan Zhong,Hefeng Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ZipBench 是一种低成本基准压缩方法（BCM），仅评估少量锚定 LLM、合成伪评估结果并以理论保证学习紧凑样本表示，选出代表性小子集；据此构建的 ZipBench Zoo 覆盖 100+ 文本、多模态、智能体任务代理基准，MAE 为 0.002–0.02，平均 Spearman 相关约 0.98。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准套件冗长、评估成本高；已有 BCM 虽可降本，但多数依赖大量 LLM 的逐样本评估结果，构建该数据本身昂贵，且难以扩展到新发布的基准。需要一种低成本的压缩方法。

Method: ZipBench：仅评估一小组锚定 LLM；合成伪评估结果以扩大覆盖；学习紧凑的样本表示；选出小而具有代表性的子集。方法具备误差与排序一致性的理论保证。

Result: 构建 ZipBench Zoo，包含 100+ 个文本、多模态与智能体任务基准代理的紧凑版本；与完整基准相比，平均绝对误差 0.002–0.02，平均 Spearman 相关约 0.98。

Conclusion: ZipBench 同时降低了 LLM 评估与紧凑基准构建的成本，降低了算力受限研究者开展广泛 LLM 研究的门槛；代码已开源。

Abstract: Comprehensive benchmark suites are essential for improving large language models (LLMs), but many widely used benchmarks are redundant, making evaluation unnecessarily expensive. Although recent benchmark compression methods (BCMs) can mitigate this cost, many strong BCMs rely on large collections of per-sample evaluation results from numerous LLMs to identify representative samples. Building such collections is also expensive unless they are already public, making these methods difficult to extend to newly released benchmarks. To address this challenge, we present ZipBench, a simple and low-cost BCM with theoretical error and rank-consistency guarantees. ZipBench evaluates only a small set of anchor LLMs, synthesizes pseudo evaluation results to broaden coverage, learns compact sample representations, and selects a small yet representative subset. Building on it, we create ZipBench Zoo, a collection of compact versions of 100+ benchmark proxies spanning text, multimodal, and agent tasks. These benchmark achieve mean absolute errors of 0.002--0.02 and average Spearman correlations of ~0.98 with the full benchmarks. Overall, ZipBench reduces the cost of both LLM evaluation and compact benchmark construction, lowering the barrier to broad LLM research for compute-constrained researchers. The code has been released in https://github.com/MilkThink-Lab/ZipBench.

</details>


### [96] [The House with a Million Windows: Interactive Fiction for Narrative Restorying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12537)
*Cody Kommers,Sarah G Immel,Drew Hemment,Mina Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于LLM的交互式小说系统HWAMW（The House with a Million Windows），借助心理学"重构故事"干预范式，让用户讲述个人故事后面对LLM生成的多种文学风格"窗口"重构版本，从而探索故事的多重意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助写作可能使人叙事的意义被"扁平化"，产出同质化内容，而缺乏写作本应有的意图性投入与意义建构过程。

Method: 设计并实现HWAMW系统：用户以文本叙事形式游玩，讲述一个故事，随后系统用不同文学风格生成一组"窗口"（重构版本）；采用实证研究评估效果，并结合专家评审分析其作用机制。

Result: 实证结果显示HWAMW能提升用户的叙事认同感（narrative identity），专家评审进一步揭示了该效果的产生方式。

Conclusion: HWAMW促进了故事重构，为AI辅助写作提供了一种有价值的新范式——LLM不替用户讲故事，而是帮助用户在自己所讲的故事中看到更大的可能性。

Abstract: AI-assisted writing can flatten meaning in human storytelling, enabling the production of homogeneous outputs without the intentional effort and sense-making writing entails. To address this challenge, we present The House with a Million Windows (HWAMW), an LLM-based interactive fiction system designed to help users explore both the breadth and depth of potential meanings within their personal stories -- drawing on a psychological paradigm called the restorying intervention. In HWAMW, users play through a text-based narrative in which they tell a story, then encounter a set of LLM-generated "windows" reframing it according to different literary styles. Empirical evidence shows that HWAMW increases users' sense of narrative identity, while an expert review explores how this effect is achieved. Our findings suggest that HWAMW facilitates restorying and offers a valuable paradigm for AI-assisted writing, wherein LLMs do not tell our stories but rather help us see greater potential in the stories we tell.

</details>


### [97] [Agent as Policy for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12541)
*Mengzhao Jia,Yang Lin,Xixin Zhang,Zhihan Zhang,Xiaobai Liu,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"Agent as Policy"(AGP)框架，让通用智能体无需任务或环境特定训练即可直接驱动机器人执行物理操作任务。智能体通过解读视觉信息、编写可执行程序、发出运动指令并依据物理反馈修正动作，实现推理与编程能力同物理世界的持续交互，在多项真实操作任务上取得高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人策略往往依赖任务特定或环境特定的训练，泛化性与自主性受限。作者希望验证通用智能体能否不经专门训练，直接充当机器人策略，将其推理与编程能力延伸到物理操作领域。

Method: 提出AGP，将任务规划与执行置于智能体控制之下。给定任务与机器人接口，智能体解读视觉证据、编写可执行程序、发出运动指令，并根据物理执行结果修正动作，形成运行时推理、编程与交互的闭环。

Result: 在涵盖精密操作、动态运动与可变形物体的多项真实世界操作任务上验证AGP，包括从人类视频学习装配、基于目标图像的积木搭建、骰子翻转、定向投掷与双臂毛巾折叠。在三种积木搭建配置上成功率分别达100%、100%和80%。

Conclusion: 研究表明通用智能体可作为机器人策略，通过运行时推理、编程与交互将其自主性扩展到物理操作，为通用智能体驱动机器人提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: We demonstrate that a general-purpose agent can directly drive a physical robot throughout task execution without any task-specific or environment-specific training. We introduce Agent as Policy (AGP), which places task planning and execution under the agent's control. Given a task and a robot interface, the agent interprets visual evidence, writes executable programs, issues motion commands, and revises its actions in response to physical outcomes. This brings the agent's reasoning and programming capabilities into continuous interaction with the physical world. We study AGP across multiple real-world manipulation tasks spanning precision manipulation, dynamic motions, and deformable objects. These include assembly from human videos, block construction from goal images, die reorientation, targeted throwing, and bimanual towel folding. AGP achieves success rates of 100%, 100%, and 80% on three block construction configurations. These findings establish a path for general-purpose agents to act as robotic policies, extending their autonomy to physical manipulation through runtime reasoning, programming, and interaction.

</details>


### [98] [Meddies-PII: A Multilingual Framework for Personally Identifiable Information Extraction in Clinical De-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12544)
*Linh Uyen Le,Christian Hoang,Huy Hoang Ha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出百万级合成临床文档数据集Meddies-PII-Dataset，覆盖17种语言、9类PII标签，通过属性条件提示生成并经13道确定性校验，训练出的BIOES分类器在15个基准上平均F1达0.827，显著优于最强基线0.658。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床去标识化依赖准确识别个人可识别信息（PII），但人工标注数据集构建成本高昂，现有合成替代方案对生成过程说明有限且合成策略较简单。

Method: 构建Meddies-PII-Dataset：100万份合成临床文档，涵盖17种语言和9类PII标签；采用属性条件提示生成，并通过13道确定性门控（deterministic gates）保证结构与标注一致性。基于该数据集训练Meddies-PII-Model（BIOES令牌分类器），与现有PII抽取系统在实体级精确匹配F1上进行比较。

Result: Meddies-PII-Model在所有报告的基准上取得最优表现，在15个外部基准上平均F1为0.827，而最强基线为0.658。

Conclusion: 该数据集与模型有效提升多语言临床去标识化性能；作者承诺录用后公开数据集、基准套件、模型、生成框架与评测代码，以支持相关研究。

Abstract: Clinical de-identification relies on accurately identifying personally identifiable information (PII). However, manually annotated datasets are costly to construct, while existing synthetic alternatives often provide limited details about their generation process or rely on relatively simple synthesis strategies. We introduce Meddies-PII-Dataset, a corpus of one million synthetic clinical documents spanning seventeen languages and nine PII labels. The documents are generated using attribute-conditioned prompts and validated through thirteen deterministic gates that enforce structural and annotation consistency. To evaluate the dataset's utility, we train Meddies-PII-Model, a BIOES token classifier, and compare it with existing PII extraction systems using exact-match entity-level F1. Meddies-PII-Model achieves the highest performance among the evaluated systems on all reported benchmarks, with a mean F1 of 0.827 across fifteen external benchmarks, compared with 0.658 for the strongest baseline. Upon acceptance, we will publicly release the dataset, benchmark suite, model, generation framework, and evaluation code to support research on multilingual clinical de-identification.

</details>


### [99] [Calibrated Ambiguity in Multimodal Language Models: Humans reach for cultural references, while models describe the picture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12575)
*Cody Kommers,Mingrui Ye,Evelyn Gius,Daniela Mihai,Hoyt Long,Zheng Yuan,Drew Hemment*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出"校准歧义"概念，并以桌游Dixit为任务，比较人类与多模态语言模型生成的线索，发现模型存在"歧义坍缩"与"文化扁平化"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI领域通常将歧义视为需要消除的缺陷，但在人类交流与文化中，歧义反而是具有生成性的资源——从幽默到政治再到艺术，人们用开放但仍有可解释边界的语言与图像表达自我。作者希望将这一"校准歧义"能力操作化，检验AI模型是否具备类似人类的模糊表达能力。

Method: 以派对游戏Dixit为实验任务，构建了一套针对"校准歧义"的新型编码评分标准（rubric），据此比较人类与多模态语言模型所生成线索的差异。

Result: 模型一致表现出"歧义坍缩"（ambiguity collapse），即输出过度具体化，未给多种合理解释留下空间；与人类线索不同，AI线索还表现出"文化扁平化"（cultural flattening），即使被提示使用典故与比喻性语言，也几乎不引用特定文化情境中的知识。

Conclusion: 校准歧义是人类交流与文化中的生成性能力，而当前多模态语言模型在这一点上存在系统性欠缺：既无法保持适度的解释开放性，也缺乏文化情境化的表达资源。

Abstract: Ambiguity is often treated as a bug for AI systems to resolve---but in human communication and culture, ambiguity can also be a generative resource. From humour to politics to art, people express themselves in words and images that are open enough to invite different interpretations, yet constrained enough to be interpretable. We operationalise this notion of calibrated ambiguity with a task drawn from the parlour game Dixit. We compare differences in clues generated by human vs multimodal language models, based on a novel coding rubric for calibrated ambiguity, and find that models consistently exhibit ambiguity collapse (i.e., their outputs are over-specified, leaving no room for multiple legitimate interpretations). Unlike human clues, AI-generated clues also exhibit cultural flattening; they almost never make reference to culturally-situated knowledge, even when prompted to use allusion and figurative language.

</details>


### [100] [SWARM: A Multilingual Human-Annotated Dataset for Russian Propaganda Detection in Search Engine Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12653)
*Manuel Tonneau,Abhinav Dubey,Farhan Shaikh,Ilaria Vitulano,Martha Stolze,Hale Dedeoglu,Clara Riechert,Ella Kuka,Maryna Sydorova,Mykola Makhortykh,Elizaveta Kuznetsova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SWARM数据集，包含九种语言、2,183条搜索引擎结果，由训练有素的标注者标注其是否支持反复出现的俄罗斯宣传叙事，并据此评测基于来源的黑名单、监督分类器与零样本大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算研究多局限于单一或少数语言的社交媒体空间，且侧重分析来源而非内容，难以刻画跨语言、跨域传播的宣传现象。

Method: 构建SWARM数据集，覆盖九种语言与多种网络域（新闻、博客、政府网站等），每条结果由人工标注是否支持特定宣传叙事；以该标注为基准，对比基于来源的黑名单、监督分类器与零样本LLM三类方法。

Result: 黑名单漏检大多数支持宣传的文档，因其并非仅出现于被标记的"宣传"媒体，也见于主流媒体；内容层面分析有所改善，最强LLM正类F1达0.73，而监督分类器仅约0.5，较小LLM则过度预测支持，将主题相关误判为立场认同。
