Conclusion: 检测搜索传播的宣传需进行分语言、内容层面的评估，作者期望SWARM与评测代码能推动该方向研究。

Abstract: Russian state propaganda spreads across many languages and online spaces. Yet, most computational work examines only one such space, usually social media, in one or two languages, and analyses sources rather than content. We introduce SWARM (Search-Web documents Annotated for Russian propaganda, Multilingual), a dataset of 2,183 search engine results across nine languages and diverse web domains (e.g., news, blogs, government sites), each annotated by trained coders for whether it supports a recurring Russian propaganda narrative. We benchmark a source-based blocklist, supervised classifiers, and zero-shot LLMs against these labels. The blocklist misses most propaganda-supporting documents, because such content is not confined to flagged "propaganda" outlets but also appears on mainstream ones. Content-level analysis helps, though how much depends on the model: the strongest LLM reaches a positive-class F1 of 0.73, whereas the supervised classifiers reach only about 0.5, with the smaller LLMs over-predicting support, mistaking topical relevance for endorsement. Detecting search-borne propaganda thus requires per-language, content-level evaluation, which we hope SWARM and our evaluation code enable.

</details>


### [101] [LifeMem: Enabling Lifelong Experience Reuse for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12655)
*Yuli Qiu,Yutong Li,Wei Su,Zeming Liu,Wanxiang Che,Heyan Huang,Haifeng Wang,Yuang Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LifeMem终身学习框架，通过聚类交互轨迹提取可复用技能，实现跨环境知识迁移并缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于记忆的LLM智能体难以跨环境迁移可复用经验，且随经验积累出现灾难性遗忘。

Method: 学习阶段基于底层工作流对累积交互轨迹聚类以提取可复用技能；推理阶段召回相关技能与轨迹以指导动作。

Result: 在10个环境、超13k任务及2k条新标注交互轨迹上验证，实现了对已学任务的遗忘减少与更优的跨任务迁移。

Conclusion: LifeMem可实现终身学习中的有效经验复用；任务流式到达会影响学习，而记忆中结构相似轨迹的整合可提升性能。

Abstract: Large language model agents are expected to continuously adapt to new tasks and environments over their lifetime by reusing past experience. However, existing memory-based agents struggle to transfer reusable experience across environments and suffer from catastrophic forgetting as experience accumulated. To address these challenges, we propose LifeMem, a lifelong learning framework that enables agents to transfer knowledge across multiple environments. During learning, LifeMem clusters accumulated interaction trajectories based on underlying workflows to extract reusable skills. When solving a new task at inference time, the agent recalls relevant skills and trajectories to guide actions. To validate our method, we conduct experiments across 10 environments and over 13k tasks with 2k newly annotated interaction trajectories. Results show that LifeMem enables effective experience reuse in lifelong learning, achieving both reduced forgetting on learned tasks and superior cross-task transfer. Further analysis reveals that task streaming impacts learning, while consolidating structurally similar trajectories within memory boosts performance.

</details>


### [102] [Doc2FRC: Length-Consistent Document-Level Machine Translation via Fixed-Range Chunking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12674)
*Xiaotian Wang,Youyuan Lin,Zhan Shen,Hitomi Yanaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出固定范围分块(FRC)策略，用动态规划将文档切分为预设长度区间内的块，统一训练与推理的长度分布，缓解长上下文LLM在文档级机器翻译中的n-gram重复与质量退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM虽可减少文档级机器翻译(DocMT)的输入截断，但直接Doc2Doc翻译易出现n-gram重复与质量逐步退化；常用的细粒度分块方案中，传统规则式分块难以应对训练与推理之间的长度分布不匹配。

Method: 提出固定范围分块(Fixed-Range Chunking, FRC)，利用动态规划把文档划分为长度落在预定义区间内的块，并在训练和推理中一致地应用，使任意长度输入映射到相同长度分布；以FRC为核心，设计轻量级双边界匹配算法用于块对齐，并给出四种训练策略。

Result: 基于FRC的微调在7B LLM上显著优于直接Doc2Doc微调，并在IWSLT2017上超越现有DocMT方法；此外构建了独立于主流DocMT训练来源的10语言测试集GlobVDoc，显示FRC改善了分布外文档翻译表现。

Conclusion: 通过训练与推理一致的固定范围分块，可有效缩小训练-测试长度失配，缓解长文档翻译的重复与退化问题，提升同分布及分布外文档级机器翻译质量。

Abstract: Advanced large language models (LLMs) with long context windows can substantially reduce input truncation in document-level machine translation (DocMT). However, direct Doc2Doc translation remains prone to n-gram repetition and progressive quality degradation. A common remedy is to segment the document into finer-grained chunks. Nonetheless, conventional rule-based chunking approaches fail to handle the length distribution mismatch between training and inference. To address this, we introduce Fixed-Range Chunking (FRC), utilizing dynamic programming to partition documents into chunks within a predefined length interval. By consistently applying FRC during training and inference, the input documents of any length are mapped to the same length distribution, substantially reducing train-test length mismatch. Centered on FRC, we propose a lightweight dual-boundary matching algorithm for chunk alignment, alongside four distinct training strategies. Experimental results show that FRC-based fine-tuning substantially improves 7B LLMs over direct Doc2Doc fine-tuning and outperforms existing DocMT methods on IWSLT2017. We further construct GlobVDoc, a 10-language test set independent of mainstream DocMT training sources, and show that FRC improves out-of-distribution document translation.

</details>


### [103] [Cognition on Graph: Navigating Massive Knowledge Space via Cognitive Cycles and Bidirectional Graph-Text Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12791)
*Gengxian Zhou,Jian Xu,Zichen Tang,Shiming Xiang,Haihong E,Cheng-Lin Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoG（Cognition on Graph），一个受认知启发、免训练的框架，用于在全局异构知识库（大规模知识图谱与文本语料）上进行自适应知识探索，以提升大语言模型在多跳问答中的复杂推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多采用反应式、图驱动的探索策略，盲目沿图拓扑展开，无法根据问题语境与探索进展自适应调整，且图与文本之间缺乏深度双向协同，导致在全局异构知识库上处理复杂推理仍具挑战性。

Method: 受人类问题求解启发，CoG 执行持续的"规划—探索—反思"循环：主动制定调查计划、执行双源检索（知识图谱与文本）、动态反思进展并调整策略；同时建立结构化图与非结构化文本的深度双向协同，由从文本中抽取的实体动态引导图探索以弥合知识缺口。

Result: 在七个多跳问答基准上的大量实验表明，CoG 显著优于现有最先进方法，同时实现了更优的探索效率。

Conclusion: CoG 作为一种认知启发、免训练的框架，通过自适应规划与图—文本双向协同，有效提升了异构知识库上的复杂推理性能与效率，代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has empowered Large Language Models (LLMs) to tackle knowledge-intensive tasks. However, navigating global, heterogeneous knowledge bases (large-scale knowledge graphs and text corpora) for complex reasoning remains a challenge. Existing methods typically employ reactive, graph-driven exploration strategies, which blindly follow graph topology without adapting to the question context or evolving exploration progress, and lack deep bidirectional synergy between graph and text. To address these limitations, we propose CoG (Cognition on Graph), a cognitive-inspired, training-free framework for adaptive knowledge exploration. Drawing inspiration from human problem-solving, CoG performs a continuous plan-explore-reflect cycle, where it proactively formulates investigation plans, performs dual-source retrieval, and dynamically reflects on progress to adjust strategies. Crucially, it establishes deep bidirectional synergy between structured graph and unstructured text, where entities extracted from text dynamically guide graph exploration to bridge knowledge gaps. Extensive experiments on seven multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate that CoG significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods while achieving superior exploration efficiency. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/zhougengxian/CoG.

</details>


### [104] [DuplexDrama: A Synthesized Dialogue Dataset with Scenarios, Full-Duplex Behaviors, Expressive Speech, and Sound Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12872)
*Qingxiang Guo,Wenke Fan,Shuofeng Zhao,Dawei Yang,Zhiyang Zhou,Yingxin Shang,Hongwei Cai,Zhou Wang,Weixu Wang,Lin Yang,Shuran Zhou,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 DuplexDrama，首个同时覆盖人格场景、三种全双工行为、人格对齐情感语音与脚本感知音效四个维度的合成口语对话数据集，含超 2000 小时音频，并将开源 6400 段双语对话（约 800 小时）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工口语对话模型研究需要同时具备丰富人格与场景设定、真实交互行为（打断、反馈、不完整语句）、情感表达及环境音效的数据支持，而现有数据集难以同时满足这四个维度。

Method: 采用 4 阶段流水线构建 DuplexDrama，并对脚本与合成音频进行质量验证；数据配置包括 64 种音色池、覆盖 13 种人格与 5 个年龄段，通过内部全双工模型训练进行验证。

Result: 产出超 2000 小时音频数据，其中 3.8% 的对话轮次至少包含一种全双工行为；将发布 6400 段双语对话的精选子集（约 800 小时，中文约 500 小时、英文约 300 小时），并提供样例页面与 LLM 评判评估提示词。

Conclusion: DuplexDrama 以多维覆盖和规模化数据为全双工口语对话模型研究提供支撑，相关精选数据与评估提示词将公开发布。

Abstract: We present DuplexDrama, the first synthesized spoken dialogue dataset that simultaneously covers four dimensions: (i) complete persona and scenario settings; (ii) three full-duplex behaviors (interruption, backchannel, incomplete); (iii) expressive speech with persona-aligned emotion labels; and (iv) script-aware sound events. DuplexDrama is built via a 4-stage pipeline; quality validation on both scripts and synthesized audio confirms its quality. We have produced more than 2,000 hours audio data with a 64-voice timbre pool spanning 13 personas and 5 age buckets; 3.8% of all turns carry at least one full-duplex behavior. This data has been validated through internal full-duplex model training. We will release a curated subset of 6,400 bilingual dialogues (800 h, Chinese ~500 h + English ~300 h) to advance full-duplex spoken dialogue model research. Data samples are available at our demo page and LLM-judge evaluation prompts will be released with the dataset.

</details>


### [105] [Parameter-Efficient Retrievers for Polish and European Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12913)
*Sławomir Dadas,Rafał Poświata,Małgorzata Grębowiec,Michał Perełkiewicz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出三段式训练流程，无需原始相关性标注，利用教师模型蒸馏出紧凑检索器 PolDense 与 EuroDense，实现强质量-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集检索依赖数十亿参数大模型，内存与算力开销使大规模索引、频繁语料更新和低延迟服务成本高昂，亟需在保持竞争力的同时压缩模型规模。

Method: 三阶段训练流程：跨语言对齐、关系知识蒸馏、对比微调；不依赖原始ground-truth相关性标签，仅使用强嵌入模型与重排器作为教师生成监督信号。PolDense为6个17M–1B参数波兰语检索器，EuroDense为435M参数、支持9种欧洲语言，均支持8192 token上下文。

Result: 在41个波兰语与150个多语言检索任务上评测：PolDense-1B优于参数量达9B的对比模型，PolDense系列在模型规模上构成Pareto前沿；1B以下模型中EuroDense在任务平均与语言平均性能均排名第一，并在9种语言中的7种领先。

Conclusion: 该流程可高效构建紧凑检索器，在不使用原始标注的前提下达到与更大模型相当的检索质量，并公开全部模型。

Abstract: Dense retrieval systems increasingly rely on multi-billion-parameter language models, whose memory and computational requirements make large-scale indexing, frequent corpus updates, and low-latency serving costly. We present a three-stage training pipeline for developing compact and efficient retrievers that remain competitive with substantially larger models. The pipeline combines cross-lingual alignment, relational knowledge distillation, and contrastive fine-tuning. It requires no original ground-truth relevance labels, relying exclusively on supervision generated by strong embedding models and rerankers utilised as teachers. Using this pipeline, we develop PolDense and EuroDense, both supporting contexts of up to 8,192 tokens. PolDense is a family of six Polish retrievers ranging from 17M to 1B parameters. EuroDense is a 435M-parameter retriever supporting nine European languages. We conduct an extensive evaluation covering 41 Polish and 150 multilingual retrieval tasks. The results demonstrate strong quality-efficiency trade-offs. PolDense-1B outperforms the evaluated retrievers with up to 9B parameters, while the PolDense family forms the Pareto frontier across model sizes. Among the evaluated models below 1B parameters, EuroDense ranks first in both task-averaged and language-averaged performance and leads in seven of nine languages. We release all models publicly.

</details>


### [106] [LLM-Enhanced Dual-Branch Learning for Large-Scale Multi-Label Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12915)
*Hui Ye,Jing Zhang,Xiulong Yang,Rajshekhar Sunderraman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DualMLC双分支框架，用自回归解码器和双向编码器分别编码同一文档并独立打分，通过后期logit融合提升大规模多标签文本分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多标签文本分类方法多依赖单一主编码器或将辅助特征并入单一排序器，未能充分挖掘异构语言模型之间的互补性。

Method: DualMLC对同一文档分别使用自回归decoder-only语言模型与双向编码器，两个分支各自保留独立表示通路并在共享标签空间上独立估计相关性分数，最后通过late logit fusion融合两个分数向量。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的大规模多标签文本分类基准上取得state-of-the-art结果；消融实验表明异构预测器融合得到的排序优于任一单分支。

Conclusion: 异构语言模型的互补证据（共享证据强化相关标签、分支特有证据互相补偿）能显著增强多标签文本分类的排序能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large-scale multi-label text classification assigns a small subset of relevant labels to each document from a vocabulary containing thousands or tens of thousands of candidate labels. Although pretrained language models have improved semantic text representations, most representation-based approaches center their prediction pipelines on a primary encoder or combine auxiliary features within a single ranker. The complementarity between heterogeneous language models therefore remains insufficiently explored. We propose DualMLC, a dual-branch framework that processes the same document through an autoregressive decoder-only language model and a bidirectional encoder. Each branch maintains its own representation pathway and independently estimates relevance scores over the shared label space. DualMLC combines the two score vectors through late logit fusion, allowing shared evidence to reinforce relevant labels and branch-specific evidence to compensate for limitations in the other branch's representation. DualMLC achieves state-of-the-art results on three widely used large-scale multi-label text classification benchmarks. Ablation results further confirm that integrating the heterogeneous predictors produces stronger rankings than either branch alone. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/huiyegit/DualMLC.

</details>


### [107] [Fewer Words, Not Fewer Tokens: Measuring the Sanskrit Tokenization Penalty per Proposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12960)
*Devansh Sharma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 梵语将格、数、人称、时态融合于词尾并以复合词串联小句，故单词信息密度高。但该密度能否在子词分词后保留，须以"命题"而非"词"为单位考察。在FLORES-200 devtest上，梵语token数是英语的1.774–2.187倍（词表≥200,019），略高于印地语（1.325–1.353倍）。以英语分词器为基准，梵语训练的BPE在当代散文上每命题成本反而低于英语（0.887）；但与同语料英语侧训练的匹配对照相比，该反转消失，32k/64k词表下8个匹配对均>1.0（95%区间不含1）。词表增大至128k时差距缩小（域内0.983、域外1.025、FLORES 1.116）。比值可分解为字符长度比与每字符token数比，后者近1；匹配分词后保留的是字符级长度。结论：在实际部署场景中，SLP1梵语相对o200k英语枢轴，每命题需1.831–2.899倍英语token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梵语因形态融合与复合构词而在词级上信息密度高，但子词分词（BPE等）可能破坏这一优势。既有比较通常以"词"为单位，掩盖了分词对不同语言造成的非对称成本。作者主张应以"命题/意义单位"为单位衡量分词后的真实成本，并检验在控制算法与词表的情况下，梵语相对英语的token化成本是真实劣势还是度量假象。

Method: 以FLORES-200 devtest为等义内容基准，统计梵语（SLP1转写）相对英语、印地语在已部署分词器（词表≥200,019）下的token比。进一步设置匹配对照：同一BPE算法与词表分别训练于同一语料的梵语侧与英语侧，在32k、64k、128k词表规模下，将各规模arm与其pair-matched及byte-matched英语对照配对，计算每命题token成本比及95%区间。将比值分解为字符长度比与每字符token数比两个因子。

Result: 未匹配时，梵语在FLORES上为英语的1.774–2.187倍token，为印地语的1.325–1.353倍；以部署英语分词器为基准，梵语BPE在当代散文上每命题成本为英语的0.887。匹配对照下该反转消失：32k与64k词表、全部8个匹配对在散文上均>1.0且95%区间不含1。128k词表时差距缩小：域内0.983、域外1.025、FLORES 1.116。比值分解显示每字符token数比全程近1，主导因子为字符长度比：SLP1梵语散文相对英语为1.028，梵语韵文则为0.596。部署场景下（SLP1梵语对o200k英语枢轴），当代散文与FLORES上梵语每命题成本为英语的1.831–2.899倍。

Conclusion: 词级信息密度不能等同于子词分词后的token效率；以命题为单位、并控制分词算法与词表后，梵语相对英语的成本劣势基本可归结为字符级长度差异而非分词器的不对称处理。稳健的结论针对实际部署实践：在当代散文与FLORES上，SLP1梵语相对o200k英语枢轴，每命题需约1.831–2.899倍英语token。代码、结果快照与全部表格均已公开。

Abstract: Sanskrit fuses case, number, person and tense into word endings and chains clauses into compounds, so it is information-dense per word. Whether that density survives subword tokenization is a separate question, to be asked per unit of meaning rather than per word. On identical FLORES-200 devtest content, Sanskrit costs 1.774-2.187 times the English tokens under deployed tokenizers with vocabularies of 200,019 ids or more, but only 1.325-1.353 times the Hindi tokens. Against a deployed English tokenizer, Sanskrit-trained BPE arms then look cheaper per proposition than English on contemporary prose (0.887). Against a matched English control, the same algorithm and vocabulary trained on the English side of the same corpus, that flip disappears: at 32,000 and 64,000 pieces all 8 matched pairs, each size-matched arm against both a pair-matched and a byte-matched control, sit above 1.0 on prose with 95% intervals excluding it. The gap closes as the vocabulary grows: at 128,000 pieces the BPE pair reads 0.983 in domain while staying above parity out of domain (1.025) and on FLORES (1.116). The ratio factorises into a character-length ratio and a tokens-per-character ratio, the second near 1 throughout: what survives matched tokenization is character-level length, which Sanskrit prose lacks over English in SLP1 (1.028) and Sanskrit verse has (0.596). The robust statement is about deployed practice: on contemporary prose and on FLORES, with the Sanskrit side in SLP1 against the deployed o200k English pivot, Sanskrit costs 1.831-2.899 English tokens per proposition under the tokenizers people actually ship. Code, the results snapshot and every table here are public.

</details>


### [108] [Judging by the Cover: Cleaning LLM Truthfulness Benchmarks to Avoid Surface-Level Feature Leakage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13003)
*Foad Namjoo,Remy Ogasawara,Amirali Abdullah,Cullen Anderson,Narmeen Fatimah Oozeer,Jeff M. Phillips*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 二元选择真值基准中，正确与错误答案在表面特征上存在系统性差异，模型可借此超越随机水平而非真正推理；本文证明该泄漏可检测、可被分类器利用，并提出清理机制Audit-Prune。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元选择真值基准要求模型在正确与错误答案间二选一，但若两类答案在表面层面特征上存在系统性差异，模型无需进行预期推理即可超过随机猜测，导致评测结果失真的"表面特征泄漏"问题。

Method: 使用简单六特征逻辑回归分类器检测TruthfulQA中正确与错误答案的可分性，验证表面伪特征在其他基准中同样存在；提出通用清理机制，通过移除最强化泄漏的答案对来降低泄漏；发布泄漏降至接近随机水平的TruthfulQA版本，并提供发布前清理工具Audit-Prune。

Result: 六特征逻辑回归分类器在TruthfulQA上区分正确与错误答案取得显著准确率；类似表面伪特征在其他基准中也被证实存在；清理后发布的TruthfulQA版本表面特征泄漏降低至接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 二元选择真值基准的表面特征泄漏是可检测、可利用的评测缺陷，会虚高模型表现；Audit-Prune机制可用于数据集发布前审计与清理，提升评测有效性。

Abstract: Binary-choice truth benchmarks ask models to choose between a correct and an incorrect answer, but if the two answers differ systematically in surface-level features, models can exceed chance without performing the intended reasoning. We show that this failure mode is detectable and can be exploited by downstream classifiers. In TruthfulQA, a simple six-feature logistic classifier achieves substantial accuracy in separating correct from incorrect answers. We further show that similar surface-level artifacts are present in additional benchmarks. To counteract this, we developed a general mechanism to clean them by removing the most leakage-reinforcing pairs. We release a version of TruthfulQA with surface-feature leakage reduced close to chance and provide a mechanism, Audit-Prune, so that the datasets can be cleaned before release.

</details>


### [109] [Tasks over Application Manuals: Revealing Gaps in Long-Horizon Procedural Reasoning for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13005)
*Utkarsh Soni,Syed Shariyar Murtaza,Yifan Nie,Sachin Chandrasekhar,Eugene Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出TAM基准，评估LLM在长时程序性推理上的能力，涵盖ICD-10-CM编码与联邦量刑两领域，结果显示最强方法准确率仅1%和15.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多为短时、少步推理，难以反映真实世界中需遵循数百页、规则相互依赖手册的可靠性；这类长时程序性任务可能被现有评测高估。

Method: 构建Tasks over Application Manuals（TAM）基准，从ICD-10-CM临床编码与美国联邦量刑（犯罪量刑指南中的罪行等级）两领域筛选真实任务，均带人工验证标签；每项任务需依据含数万条规则的权威手册，跨章节执行一系列相互依赖步骤以得出精确答案。评测通用提示方法、检索增强生成、ReAct式提示及agent-harness基线，均在GPT-5上进行。

Result: 最佳exact-match表现极低：ICD-10-CM编码约1%，量刑任务约15.5%。

Conclusion: 现有基准可能高估LLM推理能力，并遗漏关键挑战——可靠地遵循长规则化流程；TAM数据与代码已公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance on a wide range of natural language tasks, and recent benchmarks suggest that they are increasingly adept at multi-hop reasoning. However, these benchmarks are typically short-horizon, requiring only a small number of retrieval or inference steps, and provide limited evidence of reliability on real-world tasks that involve following manuals spanning hundreds of pages with complex, interdependent guidelines. In this paper, we introduce Tasks over Application Manuals (TAM), a benchmark for evaluating long-horizon procedural reasoning. We construct TAM by curating real-world tasks from two domains: ICD-10-CM clinical coding (mapping medical conditions to diagnostic codes) and U.S. federal sentencing (computing crime sentencing guideline outcomes, specifically offense levels), with human-validated labels. Each task requires following an authoritative manual with tens of thousands of rules and executing a sequence of interdependent steps across different sections to produce an exact answer. We evaluate general-purpose prompting approaches, including retrieval-augmented generation, ReAct-style prompting, and an agent-harness baseline on GPT-5, and find that the best exact-match performance remains extremely low: 1% on ICD-10-CM coding and 15.5% on sentencing tasks. These results show that current benchmarks may overestimate LLM reasoning ability and miss a key challenge: reliably following long, rule-based procedures. The complete TAM data and code are publicly available.

</details>


### [110] [Kraken: LLM-based Speech-to-Speech Translation via Low-bitrate VQ and Dual-path Source Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13045)
*Hayato Futami,Hassan Shahmohammadi,Tushar Dhyani,Alkis Koudounas,Raphaël Lafargue,Yosuke Kashiwagi,Quentin Jodelet,Emiru Tsunoo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Kraken语音到语音翻译模型：以单层VQ低比特率token重建SSL特征，配合以源语音为条件的Autowave-X解码器，基于Qwen3-8B训练，翻译质量优于SeamlessM4T-Large v2与Qwen2.5-Omni，并提升说话人及韵律迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语音LLM的S2ST存在两个瓶颈：一是LLM难以预测高比特率语音token；二是训练依赖说话人身份与韵律理想对齐的S2ST数据，数据约束强。

Method: 采用基于单层矢量量化的低比特率token，并以重建自监督学习（SSL）特征为目标进行训练；另设独立的token-to-waveform解码器Autowave-X，同时以源语音为条件以增强非语言信息迁移，从而放宽训练数据要求。在此基础上构建Kraken：在预训练LLM上增加语音特征输入与低比特率token输出，后接Autowave-X声码器；模型基于Qwen3-8B，使用15万小时多语言、多任务语音数据训练。

Result: 翻译质量优于SeamlessM4T-Large v2和Qwen2.5-Omni，同时说话人与韵律迁移能力得到提升。

Conclusion: 低比特率token+条件化解码器的组合可有效缓解语音LLM在S2ST中的预测难度与数据对齐约束，实现质量与非语言信息保留的双重改进。

Abstract: Speech-to-speech translation (S2ST) has advanced significantly with speech LLMs, offering the potential for joint optimization and preserving non-linguistic information. However, these models struggle with predicting high-bitrate speech tokens in LLMs, and face the challenge of relying on S2ST training data with ideally aligned speaker identity and prosody. We propose using low-bitrate tokens based on single-layer vector quantization, trained to reconstruct self-supervised learning (SSL) features. We also employ a separate token-to-waveform decoder named Autowave-X, which is also conditioned on the source speech to improve non-linguistic transfer, thereby relaxing the training data constraints. With the integration of these techniques, we propose an S2ST model named Kraken, which augments a pre-trained LLM with speech feature inputs and the low-bitrate token outputs, followed by Autowave-X vocoder. We built the model upon Qwen3-8B and trained it using 150k hours of multilingual and multitask speech data. We demonstrated that our model exhibited better translation quality than SeamlessM4T-Large v2 and Qwen2.5-Omni, along with improved speaker and prosody transfer capabilities.

</details>


### [111] [Expert-Space Exploration in MoE Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13058)
*Hongyi He,Zhenghao Lin,Xiao Liu,Peng Cheng,Yan Lu,Yeyun Gong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出ESRL框架，通过显式探索MoE模型的路由空间来增强强化学习后训练中的rollout多样性，在无需额外采样与计算成本下取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对MoE模型的强化学习研究主要关注优化稳定性与训练效率，将专家选择视为固定组件。然而路由决定了稀疏计算路径，进而影响输出分布，因此专家选择可作为rollout多样性的额外来源。经实证分析发现，扰动专家路由能有效改变模型输出并提升rollout多样性（类似提高解码温度），但直接扰动会激活不适配专家并显著损害rollout质量。

Method: 提出Expert-Space Exploration Reinforcement Learning (ESRL)，一种架构感知框架，显式探索MoE模型的专家路由空间。具体包括：1）保留高置信度专家作为锚点，将随机路由限制在合理候选池内，以维持可靠计算路径；2）根据路由器熵自适应调整扰动强度，避免过度扰动；3）记录rollout期间使用的专家路径，并在策略优化时重放，以缓解扰动带来的路由不匹配问题。

Result: ESRL在top-K、top-1及共享专家路由的多种MoE骨干上，以及数学、科学、代码等任务上均取得最佳性能，且不增加额外采样或计算成本。在Qwen3-30B-A3B上，ESRL优于所有对比方法，平均Pass@1和Pass@8相较GRPO分别提升3.2和4.5个百分点。对专家利用率与训练动力学的进一步分析揭示了利用MoE特定路由结构对RL训练有益的原因。

Conclusion: 通过显式利用MoE特有的路由结构，ESRL在多种路由机制与任务上实现稳定提升，验证了将专家选择作为可控多样性来源的有效性，为MoE模型的强化学习后训练提供了新的架构感知范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become central to post-training of large language models. Recent advances in RL for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have primarily focused on improving optimization stability and training efficiency, while treating the expert selection as a fixed component. Since routing determines the sparse computation paths that induce output distributions, expert selection offers an additional source of rollout diversity. Through empirical analysis, we find that perturbing expert routing effectively alters model output and increases rollout diversity, which is similar to increasing the decoding temperature. However, direct perturbation can activate unsuitable experts and substantially degrade rollout quality. Motivated by these observations, we introduce Expert-Space Exploration Reinforcement Learning (ESRL), an architecture-aware framework that explicitly explores the expert-routing space of MoE models. ESRL preserves high-confidence experts as anchors, and restricts stochastic routing to a plausible candidate pool, thereby retaining reliable computation paths. The perturbation strength is further adapted according to router entropy to avoid over-perturbation. To mitigate the routing mismatch introduced by perturbation, ESRL records the expert paths used during rollout and replays them during policy optimization. Experiments demonstrate that ESRL achieves the best performance across MoE backbones with top-K, top-1, and shared-expert routing, as well as across mathematics, science, and code tasks without additional sampling or computational cost. Specifically, ESRL on Qwen3-30B-A3B achieves the best among all compared methods, improving average Pass@1 and Pass@8 over GRPO by 3.2 and 4.5 percentage points, respectively. Further analyses of expert utilization and training dynamics provide insights into how exploiting MoE-specific routing structure benefits RL training.

</details>


### [112] [Continue, Adapt, or Yield: In-Turn Adaptation to Overlapping Speech in Full-Duplex Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13117)
*Yunqi Lu,Tyler Baumgartner,Nikhil Johri,Brandon Tai,Candice Fan,Luc Debaupte,Ruben Aguilar,Bill Wang,Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Duplex Cue，一个评估全双工语音智能体“话轮内适应”能力的基准，将听者意图（反馈、协作、打断）与说话者行为（不变、适应、让出）分开建模，并通过单模型案例研究比较人类与 PersonaPlex 的响应差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工评估多采用二元判断（继续说 or 停止），无法刻画人类常用的第三种响应：在继续说下去的同时把听者刚提供的内容（补充词、纠正、澄清）纳入话语。这种“话轮内适应”是自然语音交互的关键，但缺乏相应评测手段。

Method: 提出 Duplex Cue 评测框架：将听者意图（backchannel、collaboration、interruption）与说话者行为（continuing unchanged、adapting within the turn、yielding）解耦，且适应包含确认性回应与内容修订。在单模型案例研究中，从无脚本英语对话中取 300 条经人工确认的 cue，重放听者音频并生成 PersonaPlex 续说，与人类真实响应对照。保留 208 对在 cue 起始时说话者仍活跃且两种条件下均有可评分响应的样本。

Result: 在 66 对协作型样本上，真实人类说话者有 68.2% 出现适应，而 PersonaPlex 仅 34.8%；模型其余情况为保持不变（42.4%）或让出话轮（22.7%）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，评估自然语音交互不能只看智能体是否继续说，还必须测量其如何回应听者的贡献；“话轮内适应”应成为全双工语音智能体评测的核心维度。

Abstract: Full-duplex evaluation often emphasizes whether an agent keeps speaking or stops. That binary cannot express a third response humans use routinely: continuing to speak while incorporating what the listener just contributed. The contribution may be a missing word, a correction or a clarification. We introduce Duplex Cue, an evaluation of this \emph{in-turn adaptation} in full-duplex voice agents. Duplex Cue separates listener intent (backchannel, collaboration, or interruption) from speaker behavior: continuing unchanged, adapting within the turn, or yielding. Adaptation includes acknowledgment as well as content revision. In a single-model case study using 300 human-confirmed cues from unscripted English conversations, we compare recorded human responses with PersonaPlex continuations generated while replaying the listener's audio. We retain 208 pairs with the ongoing speaker active at cue onset and a scorable response in each condition. On the 66 collaborative pairs, recorded speakers adapt in 68.2\% of cases, compared with 34.8\% for PersonaPlex. The model otherwise continues unchanged (42.4\%) or yields (22.7\%). These findings show why evaluating natural voice interaction requires measuring how an agent responds to a listener's contribution as well as whether it keeps speaking.

</details>


### [113] [SAS: Simple Attention Sparsification via End-to-End Optimization of Context Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13141)
*Zhiwei Li,Lei Zhu,Hao Gu,Xiang Hu,Yan Wang,Haitao Mi,Sirui Han,Leo Liang,Zhijiang Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SAS是一种门控稀疏注意力机制，将选择器的连续分数注入注意力logits，用语言建模损失端到端优化上下文排序，在多个任务上优于可训练稀疏注意力基线，预算紧张时增益尤大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练式稀疏注意力方法用轻量选择器打分后再做硬Top-K选择，导致梯度被阻断，通常只能蒸馏逐层稠密注意力分布。这种排序虽让选择器拟合原模型的注意力权重，却未直接对齐"固定注意力预算下上下文单元对预测的实际影响"，可能把有限预算浪费在用处较小的单元上。

Method: 提出Simple Attention Sparsification (SAS)，一种门控稀疏注意力机制，用语言建模损失端到端优化上下文排序。核心是把选择器的连续分数注入训练时的注意力logits，使损失可通过标准反向传播更新选择器。关键设计选择包括：将门放在注意力softmax内部的log形式；使用归一化的softmax门来校准历史上下文与始终保留的当前block；保留连续选择器分数，使模型学习相对优先级而非仅硬选择。为支持长序列训练，实现了一个内存高效的Triton kernel，将SAS集成到FlashAttention风格计算中。

Result: 在推理、长上下文理解和智能体任务上，SAS在不同注意力预算下一致优于可训练稀疏注意力基线，在紧张预算下提升尤为显著，表明其上下文排序对下游任务更有效。

Conclusion: 通过将门控连续分数注入注意力logits并以语言建模损失端到端训练，SAS解决了硬Top-K选择与预测影响之间的排序错位问题，配合内存高效的Triton实现，在多种任务和预算条件下取得优于基线的一致效果。

Abstract: Post-training attention sparsification reduces the quadratic cumulative attention cost of pretrained Transformers by selecting a small set of context units (tokens or blocks) for each query. Existing trainable methods usually use a lightweight selector to score context units, followed by hard Top-K selection that blocks gradients from the language modeling loss. Consequently, these methods commonly distill layer-wise dense attention distributions. Although this encourages the selector to rank context units by dense attention weights in the original model, the ranking is not directly aligned with their impact on predictions under a fixed attention budget (i.e., the number of attended context units per query), potentially wasting the limited budget on less useful units. To address this misalignment, we propose Simple Attention Sparsification (SAS), a gated sparse attention mechanism that optimizes context ranking end-to-end with the language modeling loss. The key idea is to inject the selector's continuous scores into attention logits during training, allowing the loss to update the selector through standard backpropagation. We identify several choices crucial for this simple design to work well in practice: placing the gate inside the attention softmax in log form, using normalized softmax gates to calibrate historical context against the always-retained current block, and preserving continuous selector scores so the model learns relative priorities rather than only hard selections. To support long-sequence training, we implement a memory-efficient Triton kernel that integrates SAS into FlashAttention-style computation. Across reasoning, long-context understanding, and agentic tasks, SAS consistently outperforms trainable sparse attention baselines across attention budgets, with especially large gains under tight budgets, demonstrating more effective context ranking for downstream tasks.

</details>


### [114] [Type Diversity Enables Transformers to Generalise Compositionally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13144)
*Anssi Moisio,Mathias Creutz,Mikko Kurimo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出，Transformer在结构泛化上比词汇泛化更难并非模型固有缺陷，而是由既有数据集中词汇类型多样性高、结构类型多样性低造成的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作发现结构泛化比词汇泛化更难，并常将其归因于Transformer架构本身。作者认为这一差异可能源于数据集属性而非模型，即先前数据集中的类型多样性（type diversity）分布不均：词汇类型多样而结构类型单一。

Method: 以“构造器数量”定义类型多样性，通过Grammatical Framework构建COGS和SLOG的语言学多样化变体，系统改变词汇与结构类型的多样性水平，并在这些变体上检验泛化表现。

Result: 类型多样性与组合泛化能力的相关性在词汇和结构测试用例中同等显著，支持作者假设。结果与“复合散度（compound divergence）解释组合泛化难度”的既有论断相矛盾。

Conclusion: 结构泛化更难并非Transformer的固有属性，而是数据集类型多样性的产物；此外，其他类型多样性与逻辑语义格式的表面属性也会影响组合泛化，需在评估中进一步考察。

Abstract: Compositional generalisation has been divided into lexical and structural generalisation. Previous work has found that structural generalisation is harder than lexical for Transformers. We propose that this difference is not inherent to Transformers, but due to the high diversity of lexical types and low diversity of structural types in the specific datasets of these previous works. By type diversity we mean the number of different constructors of that type, instead of, for example, the specific word combinations that might populate the structure. To test this, we vary the amounts of type diversity of lexical and structural types in previously published datasets. We create linguistically diverse variants of the COGS and SLOG datasets using Grammatical Framework. We find that type diversity correlates with compositional generalisation equally in lexical and structural test cases, supporting our hypothesis. We note a contradiction with the proposition in previous work that compound divergence explains the difficulty in compositional generalisation tasks. We further investigate the effects of other dataset properties on compositional generalisation, such as the diversity of types other than the novel test structure, and surface properties of the logical semantics format.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Occamy-1.0: Open Pareto-frontier 35B Intelligence for Co-work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11977)
*Wenhui Chen,Shiwen Cheng,Hao Dong,Chenda Duan,Ruixiang Feng,Zhong Guan,Boqiang Guo,Xueyuan Han,Haojie Hao,Liangmeng Huang,Zhelong Huang,Xinke Kong,Hongyu Li,Jiazheng Li,Junbo Li,Qingchuan Li,Yukun Lian,Chang Liu,Tianyu Liu,Zicheng Liu,Shuyi Ouyang,Yijun Pan,Kunyu Shi,Xiaojun Tang,Bingquan Wang,Kesu Wang,Yuchen Wang,Sibo Wei,Sicong Xie,Xiaoying Xing,Yi Xu,Zhijun Xu,Hongwei Xue,Qingcheng Zeng,Di Zhang,Guannan Zhang,Haochen Zhang,Tianlong Zhang,Tianyu Zhao,Tianyu Zhao,Yanjun Zheng,Jialong Zhu,Zijian Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Occamy-1.0，一个基于 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 后训练检查点继续训练得到的低成本协作（co-work）模型，通过执行接地数据、可回放长程轨迹与分阶段后训练提升执行效率，在多个基准上达到同规模最强并接近更大前沿模型，处于成本-性能帕累托前沿的低成本拐点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作智能体需在多次模型调用中完成信息搜集、工具使用、编程与文件操作等复杂工作流，成本与延迟会随整个回合累积。因此其实用价值不仅取决于峰值能力，也取决于能力交付的效率。而日常工作中许多步骤侧重状态跟踪、协调、恢复与任务跟进，而非前沿规模的推理能力，这为构建高性价比模型提供了空间。

Method: 以已后训练的 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 检查点为起点进一步训练；构建执行接地（execution-grounded）的数据与环境；在多种 harness 下采集可回放的长程轨迹；采用分阶段后训练（staged post-training）来发展并巩固互补的执行能力。

Result: 在广泛的协作基准套件上，Occamy-1.0 始终位居同规模模型中最强之列，并在若干任务上与体量显著更大的前沿系统保持竞争力。在设定评测与定价协议下，其在四个代表性基准上的综合表现位于观测到的成本-性能帕累托前沿的低成本拐点。工具调用、编程与指令遵循的辅助评测显示该专精化并未损害广泛的智能体能力。

Conclusion: 该工作表明面向执行效率的专精化后训练可在控制成本的同时保持广泛智能体能力，为实用协作智能体与智能体后训练研究提供了模型权重与部分训练数据支持。

Abstract: Co-work agents execute complex workflows that combine information gathering, tool use, coding, and file manipulation across many model invocations. Because cost and latency accumulate over the full episode, their practical value depends not only on peak capability but also on how efficiently that capability is delivered. Yet many steps in everyday work emphasize state tracking, coordination, recovery, and follow-through rather than frontier-scale reasoning. We present Occamy-1.0, a cost-efficient co-work model obtained by further training the post-trained Qwen3.6-35B-A3B checkpoint. We construct execution-grounded data and environments, capture replayable long-horizon trajectories across multiple harnesses, and use staged post-training to develop and consolidate complementary execution capabilities. Across a broad suite of co-work benchmarks, Occamy-1.0 is consistently among the strongest comparably sized models and remains competitive with substantially larger frontier systems on several tasks. Under our stated evaluation and pricing protocol, its aggregate performance across four representative benchmarks places it at the low-cost knee of the observed cost--performance Pareto frontier. Supporting evaluations in tool calling, coding, and instruction following further show that this specialization preserves broad agentic capability. We release the model weights and a subset of the training data to support research on practical co-work agents and agentic post-training.

</details>


### [116] [Reading the Whole Heart: Latent-Attention Masked Autoencoders for Multimodal Cardiac Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12035)
*Andrea Agostini,Simon Böhi,Moritz Vandenhirtz,Samuel Ruiperez-Campillo,Max Krähenmann,Silke Mühlstedt,Irene Cannistraci,Ece Özkan Elsen,Julia E. Vogt,Thomas M. Sutter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出Latent-Attention Masked Autoencoders (LAMAE)，一种多模态、结构感知的掩码自编码器，在自监督预训练阶段通过共享潜在注意力模块联合学习患者级表征，在MIMIC-IV多模态任务上超越模态特定预训练与强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管诊断依赖ECG、超声心动图、胸片和临床变量等互补模态的整合，各模态捕捉心脏生理不同但相关的方面。然而多数医学基础模型仍是模态特定的，仅在微调或后训练阶段组合模态，这丢弃了临床医生自然整合的跨模态证据，也忽略了每个模态内部的结构。

Method: LAMAE是一种多模态、结构感知的掩码自编码器，在自监督预训练中联合学习患者级表征。其不在事后融合模态，而是通过一个共享的潜在注意力模块，在study-view-entity层级结构上直接在潜在空间交换信息，从而聚合变量观测并优雅处理缺失模态。

Result: 在超过120万次MIMIC-IV住院记录上预训练后，LAMAE在多模态住院任务（院内死亡率、ICD-10与DRG编码、住院时长）上优于模态特定预训练以及强对比学习和视觉-语言基线，在单模态任务上保持竞争力；即使测试时仅有单一模态可用，这些增益依然存在。

Conclusion: 同时建模模态内与模态间结构可产生更鲁棒、更可迁移的表征，验证了在自监督预训练阶段进行潜在空间跨模态信息交换的有效性。

Abstract: Cardiovascular diagnosis rests on integrating complementary modalities, like ECG, echocardiography, chest radiographs, and clinical variables, each capturing distinct but correlated aspects of cardiac physiology. Yet most medical foundation models remain modality-specific, combining modalities only for finetuning or post-training. This discards the cross-modal evidence clinicians naturally integrate and ignores the structure within each modality. We introduce Latent-Attention Masked Autoencoders (LAMAE), a multimodal, structure-aware masked autoencoder that jointly learns patient-level representations during self-supervised pretraining. Rather than fusing modalities post hoc, LAMAE exchanges information directly in the latent space through a shared latent-attention module operating over a study-view-entity hierarchy, enabling aggregation of variable observations and graceful handling of missing modalities. Pretrained on over 1.2 million MIMIC-IV hospital stays, LAMAE outperforms modality-specific pretraining and strong contrastive and vision-language baselines across multimodal hospital-stay tasks, such as in-hospital mortality, ICD-10 and DRG coding, and length of stay, while remaining competitive on unimodal tasks. These gains persist even when only a single modality is available at test time, showing that modeling both intra- and inter-modal structure yields more robust, transferable representations.

</details>


### [117] [DU-NO: A Parameter-Efficient Double U-Shaped Neural Operator for Phase-Resolving Wave Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12115)
*Enrique Hernandez Noguera,Md Meftahul Ferdaus,Nathan Cooper,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出DU-NO（Double U-shaped Neural Operator），一种多尺度U形谱算子，仅在编码器和解码器最浅的两层附加轻量卷积U-Net分支，以3.64M参数在FUNWAVE-TVD近岸波浪基准上取得最优自回归误差，较U-FNO提升14.9%且参数少10.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FUNWAVE-TVD等相位解析波浪模型虽精度高，但计算成本难以支撑集合预报、不确定性量化和实时预警。神经算子可望以低成本达到求解器级精度，但在波浪主导场上，高精度模型（如U-FNO）参数量高达数千万，规模过大。

Method: 提出DU-NO（Double U-shaped Neural Operator），一种多尺度U形谱算子，仅在编码器与解码器最浅的两层接入轻量卷积U-Net分支。该设计基于采样论证：高波数成分仅存在于细网格上，因此局部全频带通路被放置在高频信息所在处，而粗尺度、带限的层级保持纯谱结构。采用随深度衰减的模态调度，将模型限制在3.64M参数，比U-FNO低一个数量级。

Result: 在公开的FUNWAVE-TVD基准上，DU-NO在六种相同训练架构中取得最佳自回归推演误差，较U-FNO提升14.9%且参数少10.8倍；频带分析显示增益覆盖所有频带，包括截断谱算子失效的高波数带。参数匹配对照（统一至3.6M预算）表明增益源于架构设计，最佳基线仍落后DU-NO 28.6%。该优势可迁移：在二维Navier-Stokes上匹配最强基线，在PDEBench浅水推演上明显胜出。代码、训练模型与评估工件已公开。

Conclusion: DU-NO通过将卷积分支仅置于浅层、保持深层纯谱的架构设计，在显著降低参数量的同时提升了波浪主导场的预测精度，验证了架构本身带来的增益，并展现出跨任务的泛化能力。

Abstract: Phase-resolving wave models such as FUNWAVE-TVD are the accuracy standard for nearshore dynamics, resolving the shoaling, refraction, and breaking of individual waves, but their cost rules them out for the ensembles, uncertainty quantification, and real-time warning that operational forecasting demands. Neural operators promise solver-level accuracy at a fraction of that cost, yet on wave-dominated fields the accurate ones are large: hybrid spectral-convolutional operators such as U-FNO (the strongest baseline in our study after DU-NO) buy their fidelity with tens of millions of parameters. We introduce DU-NO (Double U-shaped Neural Operator), a multiscale U-shaped spectral operator that attaches lightweight convolutional U-Net branches only at its two shallowest encoder and decoder levels. The placement follows a sampling argument: high-wavenumber content exists only on fine grids, so the local, full-band pathways go where that content lives, while the coarse, band-limited levels stay purely spectral. A depth-decaying mode schedule holds the model to 3.64M parameters, an order of magnitude below U-FNO. On our publicly released FUNWAVE-TVD benchmark, DU-NO attains the best autoregressive rollout error of six identically trained architectures, improving on U-FNO by 14.9% with 10.8x fewer parameters, and a frequency-band analysis shows the gain holds across all bands, including the high-wavenumber band where truncated-spectral operators collapse. Parameter-matched controls confirm the gain is architectural: rescaled to the same 3.6M budget, the best baseline still trails DU-NO by 28.6%. The advantage carries beyond nearshore waves: DU-NO matches the strongest baselines on 2D Navier-Stokes and wins clearly on PDEBench shallow-water rollouts. Code, trained models, and evaluation artifacts are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/duno-code-5A7B/.

</details>


### [118] [Can LLMs in Draft-Verify-Revise Pipelines Resolve Deictic Ambiguity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12162)
*Obinna I. Ekekezie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究草稿-验证-修订（draft-verify-revise）流水线中的"指示性漂移"现象：同一上下文依赖表达（如"previous"）在不同阶段被不同LLM解读为不同指称对象，进而影响meta-evaluator的裁决准确性。作者用小型合成数据集测试6个模型、21种推理强度配置，发现平衡准确率跨度极大（0.156至近乎完美），并指出模型失败时多依赖表面线索而非可操作推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着推理时计算扩展，草稿-验证-修订成为常见LLM编排模式，但上下文在阶段间级联传递时，不同阶段的模型可能对"previous"等上下文依赖表达给出不同解读，产生指示性漂移。这一现象对流水线的正确性构成潜在威胁，但目前缺乏系统性的实证研究，因此需要量化其影响并给出工程建议。

Method: 构建包含10个基础样例、每个样例3种条件的合成数据集：保持共享成分不变，仅改变是草稿阶段LLM（助手）还是验证阶段LLM（评分者）正确解析该表达，以及修订阶段LLM（元评估者）判定正确读法所需的独立推理量。测试来自3家提供商的6个模型、21种推理强度配置，使用e-values进行序贯检验，并设置主实验与消融实验（从评分者反馈中移除错误分类标签）。另用独立LLM分析元评估者每次错误裁决的陈述理由。

Result: 平衡准确率（敏感度与特异度的非加权均值）跨度从0.156（低于随机）到接近完美。GPT-5.2从无推理时的0.156升至最高推理强度下的0.942；Gemini 3 Pro在所有强度下均保持0.94以上，且其低推理强度表现优于GPT-5.2的xhigh强度，每次试验成本约为后者的5%。元评估者出错时，倾向于依赖表面线索而非可操作推理。

Conclusion: 指示性漂移会实质性地破坏 draft-verify-revise 流水线的可靠性，且推理强度的收益因模型而异。实施该范式的上下文工程师应警惕指示性漂移，并在每个阶段显式声明预期指称对象，以降低跨阶段解读分歧带来的风险。

Abstract: Draft-verify-revise is a common LLM orchestration pattern for scaling inference-time compute. One LLM drafts, a second critiques the draft and provides feedback, and a third uses that feedback to revise the draft into the final output. As context cascades between stages, LLMs at different stages can resolve a context-dependent expression such as "previous" differently. When that happens, the expression undergoes a deictic shift, a change in what it refers to. This phenomenon was studied with a synthetic dataset of 10 base examples, each rendered in three conditions. Holding the shared components constant, the conditions varied whether the draft stage LLM (the assistant) or the verify stage LLM (the grader) resolved the expression correctly, and how much independent reasoning the revise stage LLM (the meta-evaluator) needed to determine which reading was correct. Six models from three providers were tested across 21 reasoning effort configurations using e-values for sequential testing, in a primary experiment and an ablation experiment that removed error classification labels from the grader's feedback. A separate LLM analyzed the meta-evaluator's stated rationale for each wrong verdict. Balanced accuracy (the unweighted mean of sensitivity and specificity) ranged from 0.156, below chance, to near-perfect. GPT-5.2 rose from 0.156 without reasoning to 0.942 at its highest reasoning effort level, while Gemini 3 Pro stayed above 0.94 at every level. Gemini 3 Pro at low reasoning effort outscored GPT-5.2 at xhigh reasoning effort for roughly 5% of the cost per trial. When the meta-evaluator erred, it tended to rely on surface cues rather than operational reasoning. Context engineers implementing draft-verify-revise pipelines should be wary of deictic shifts and make the intended referent explicit at each stage.

</details>


### [119] [GLARE: Generative Learning via Adversarial Reward Estimation For Social Dynamics Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12165)
*Tenghao Huang,Zhaoxuan Tan,Muhao Chen,Jonathan May,Mengting Wan,Longqi Yang,Pei Zhou,Sihao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出会议动态预测基准MDFB（基于2,207场真实会议、24,794条面向未来的查询），要求模型根据转录前缀和活跃问题一次性生成多轮后续对话，并从效用性和拟人性两个维度评估；同时提出GLARE（将对抗性模仿学习适配到条件语言生成），在人类评估中取得0.66效用、0.70拟人性的平均胜率，优于SFT和SPIN但低于真实人类续写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 会议延续需要跟踪议程、发言者角色、参与者意图以及长时间多方讨论中的分歧，但现有工作缺乏面向未来、多轮且不强求复现精确未来的评估设定，也缺少针对会议行为的学习与输出评估手段。

Method: 构建MDFB基准（2,207场真实会议、24,794条面向未来查询），设定"给定转录前缀+活跃问题→一次性生成合理多轮延续"的任务；评估不要求复现观测未来，而衡量效用性（对问题的推进）与拟人性（对话流畅度与角色一致性）。提出GLARE：将对抗性模仿学习适配到条件语言生成，判别器将观测续写排在当前actor样本之上，其分数作为KL正则化策略奖励，并通过在当前策略负样本上重训练使奖励地形随actor演化。

Result: GLARE在人类评估中的平均胜率为效用性0.66、拟人性0.70，优于SFT和SPIN，但仍低于真实人类续写。此外，MDFB可作为社会推理竞技场，通过参考辅助判断比较通用模型（含闭源系统）。

Conclusion: 该基准兼具两项用途：面向特定任务的学习，以及基于输出的会议行为评估，为会议动态预测与通用模型社会推理能力比较提供了统一平台。

Abstract: Meeting continuation requires tracking the agenda, speaker roles, participant intentions, and disagreement across long multi-party discussions. We introduce the Meeting Dynamic Forecasting Benchmark (MDFB), constructed from 2,207 real-world meetings and 24,794 future-facing queries. Given a transcript prefix and an active question, a model generates a plausible multi-turn continuation in one call. We evaluate utility---progress toward the question---and human-likeness---plausible conversational flow and role consistency---without requiring exact reproduction of the observed future. We further present GLARE, an adaptation of adversarial imitation learning to conditional language generation. A discriminator ranks the observed continuation above samples from the current actor, and its score supplies a KL-regularized policy reward; retraining on current-policy negatives allows the reward landscape to evolve with the actor. GLARE attains average human-evaluated win rates of 0.66 on utility and 0.70 on human-likeness, outperforming SFT and SPIN while remaining below the observed human continuation. We also demonstrate MDFB as a social reasoning arena for comparing general-purpose models, including closed-source systems, through reference-assisted judgments. Together, these studies illustrate the benchmark's use for both task-specific learning and output-based evaluation of meeting behavior.

</details>


### [120] [Soft Symbol Grounding for Prototypical Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12247)
*Marcos Galván-López,Nijesh Upreti,Hiram Calvo,Carlos Aguilar-Ibáñez,Vaishak Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Soft-PNet，将概念接地重构为基于可行性符号解缓存的 Metropolis 游走，用统一 KL 目标替代任务特定的可微损失，在多个任务上匹配甚至优于损失工程化的原型网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号模型仅在最终标签上监督，中间概念不可观测，导致“推理捷径”：预测标签正确却恢复错误概念。原型网络通过将概念锚定在少量标注样本上缓解该问题，但现有方法仍需人为设计、任务特定的可微损失来耦合感知与推理，每换一个任务就要重新设计损失。

Method: Soft-PNet 去掉该手工损失：把概念接地重述为在预计算的可行符号解缓存上的 Metropolis 游走，由每个概念单个标注锚点构建的原型分布引导；训练仅使用一个 KL 目标，对齐“原型加权的缓存分布”与网络的概念预测。该目标跨任务一致，且在解空间无法枚举时依然适用。

Result: 在 MNIST-EvenOdd、Visual Sudoku 和 Kand-Logic 上、稀缺监督设置下，Soft-PNet 在概念层与标签层均达到损失工程化原型网络的水平，并恢复了软接地基线遗漏的概念，同时无需损失工程且训练时间更短。

Conclusion: Soft-PNet 以统一的 KL 目标替代任务特定可微损失，实现跨任务一致、可扩展的概念接地，在稀缺监督下兼具准确性与效率，为缓解推理捷径提供了无需损失工程的通用方案。

Abstract: Neuro-symbolic models are usually trained with supervision only on final labels, leaving the intermediate concepts unobserved. Since many concept assignments are consistent with a given label, training can predict labels correctly while recovering the wrong concepts, a failure known as a reasoning shortcut. Prototypical networks reduce shortcuts by anchoring each concept to a few labeled examples, but existing methods still couple perception and reasoning through a hand-crafted, task-specific differentiable loss that must be redesigned for every task. We introduce \textbf{Soft-PNet}, which removes this loss: it reframes concept grounding as a Metropolis walk over a precomputed cache of feasible symbolic solutions, guided by a prototype distribution built from a single labeled anchor per concept, and trains against one KL objective between the prototype-weighted cache and the network's concept predictions. The objective is identical across tasks and remains applicable when the solution space cannot be enumerated. On \texttt{MNIST-EvenOdd}, Visual Sudoku, and \texttt{Kand-Logic} under scarce supervision, Soft-PNet matches loss-engineered prototypical networks at the concept and label levels and recovers concepts that soft-grounding baselines miss, with no loss engineering and lower training time.

</details>


### [121] [Learning Symbolic Constraint Representations from Examples: A Neuro-Symbolic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12267)
*Nassim Belmecheri,Arnaud Gotlieb,Nadjib Lazaar,Helge Spieker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种神经-符号框架用于自动约束获取（CA），通过神经Oracle Transformer模拟用户响应并泛化概念知识，与FastCA引擎交互，将预测转化为可解释的约束网络，从而减少人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束获取方法依赖与人类oracle的大量交互，学习过程在时间和查询数量上代价高昂。

Method: 引入神经Oracle Transformer模型以模拟用户回答并泛化概念知识；该学习到的oracle与专用CA引擎FastCA交互，FastCA将oracle的响应系统性地精炼为可靠、一致且可解释的约束网络。

Result: 神经-符号交互有效对齐了数据驱动的模式识别与符号推理。

Conclusion: 该框架为组合域中模型构建的自动化提供了稳健途径，可在无先验领域知识的情况下从数据中恢复结构化符号模型。

Abstract: Learning user-defined concepts as constraint networks has been extensively studied in the constraint acquisition (CA) literature. However, existing approaches typically rely on intensive interactions with a human oracle, making the learning process costly in terms of time and number of queries. In this paper, we propose a neuro-symbolic framework for automatic CA that significantly reduces user involvement by introducing neural Oracle Transformer models which learn to emulate user responses and to generalize conceptual knowledge. Trained on previously available examples, the learned oracle interacts with a dedicated CA engine, FastCA, which systematically refines the oracle's responses into a sound, consistent, and interpretable constraint network. This neuro-symbolic interaction enables the recovery of structured symbolic models from data without prior domain knowledge. Our results demonstrate that this neuro-symbolic interplay effectively aligns data-driven pattern recognition with symbolic reasoning, offering a robust approach to automating model construction in combinatorial domains.

</details>


### [122] [Robust Prototypical Networks for Few-Shot Sensor Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12287)
*Mohammed Ayalew Belay,Amirshayan Haghipour,Pierluigi Salvo Rossi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多片段原型网络（MEPN），通过聚合多个不相交支持片段的原型均值来降低极低样本情形下原型方差，在DeFACTO传感器故障数据集上显著优于单片段基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业故障诊断常仅有少量带标签故障样本，标准原型网络虽简单有效，但在极低样本（very-low-shot）情形下，由于每个决策仅依赖小规模支持集，类原型不稳定。

Method: MEPN聚合来自多个不相交支持片段的原型，以其均值作为最终类代表，在不改变编码器架构的前提下降低原型方差；在DeFACTO传感器数据集上进行五类故障分类，注入合成偏置、漂移、尖峰和噪声故障，采用每片段单样本设置（K=1，聚合N_agg=10个支持片段）。

Result: 100次独立运行中，MEPN在单样本（K=1、N_agg=10）设置下达到SensorOneShotGcpn%的精度，显著高于单片段基线；在等量10样本支持预算下，MEPN与K=10的ProtoNet统计上不可区分。

Conclusion: 性能提升来源于原型累积机制而非更优的固定预算学习能力；MEPN为少样本工业故障诊断提供了一种无需修改编码器的简单有效方案。

Abstract: Industrial fault diagnosis often operates with only a handful of labeled fault examples, making few-shot learning attractive for sensor monitoring. Standard prototypical networks are simple and effective; however, their class prototypes may become unstable in the very-low-shot regime because each decision relies on a small support set. We propose \emph{Multi-Episode Prototypical Networks} (MEPN), which aggregate prototypes from multiple disjoint support episodes and use their mean as the final class representative, reducing prototype variance without changing the encoder architecture. We evaluate MEPN on the DeFACTO sensor dataset using five-way fault classification with synthetic bias, drift, spike, and noise faults injected into real industrial measurements. Over 100 independent runs, MEPN reaches \textbf{\SensorOneShotGcpn\%} in the per-episode one-shot setting ($K\!=\!1$ shot, aggregated over $N_{\text{agg}}\!=\!10$ support episodes), substantially above single-episode baselines. Under an equal 10-sample support budget, MEPN and ProtoNet at $K\!=\!10$ are statistically indistinguishable, confirming prototype accumulation as the mechanism rather than superior fixed-budget learning.

</details>


### [123] [Do Influence-Derived Data Perturbations Enable Machine Unlearning? A Controlled Study of Three Plausible Roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12313)
*Chenkai Wu,Chrispine Kambimbi,Qinyang Zeng,Jun Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在三种角色下独立评估DPL（深度扰动学习）在机器遗忘中的效果，通过审计公开实现并修正两项正确性错误后，发现DPL在直接删除、效用正则化和对抗性遗忘热启动三种设定下均无优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作把DPL分别定位为直接删除信号、效用保持正则化和对抗性遗忘的热启动，但较弱角色的证据常被用来支持最强的删除主张；作者认为需要将三种角色分离，在匹配协议下逐一检验，并审查公开实现的正确性。

Method: 在统一匹配协议下，以精确同种子重训练作为基线，分别测试DPL的三种角色。同时审计公开代码：发现图像扰动方向在增强且归一化的张量上计算、却应用于原始图像，且标签扰动低于float32分辨率、实际未改变标签。修正图像扰动流程后，在CIFAR-10/ResNet-18上进行三组配对种子实验，并在Tiny ImageNet上做单种子检验。

Result: 修正后DPL在三组配对种子上均未通过直接删除标准；其效用效应符号在不同种子间不一致；计入方向计算时间后，表现不如简单的热启动基线。Tiny ImageNet单种子结果同样不支持DPL作为正则化或热启动，且发布代码的预处理不一致使该处直接比较无法定论。

Conclusion: DPL作为机器遗忘方法的三项主张均缺乏支持；结论仅覆盖随机实例删除这一设定，不排除影响函数类方法在其他删除机制下的可能。作者发布了角色匹配的评估协议和针对扰动式删除主张的审计清单。

Abstract: We evaluate Deep Perturbation Learning (DPL), which perturbs training images and labels along influence-derived directions, in three roles in which prior work has positioned it for machine unlearning: a direct deletion signal (the strongest claim), a utility-preserving regularizer, and a warm start for adversarial unlearning. Evidence for the weaker roles has been used to support the stronger one, so we test each role separately under a matched protocol with exact-seed retraining baselines. An audit of the public implementation identifies two correctness issues: image directions are computed on augmented, normalized tensors but applied to raw images, and the label perturbation falls below float32 resolution, leaving labels unchanged. After correcting the image-perturbation pipeline, DPL fails the direct-deletion criterion on CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 in all three paired seeds. Its utility effects are inconsistent in sign across seeds, and once direction-computation time is counted it underperforms simple warm-start baselines. A one-seed Tiny ImageNet check likewise does not favor DPL as a regularizer or warm start; preprocessing inconsistencies in the released code make the direct comparison there inconclusive. These results cover random instance deletion only and do not rule out influence-based methods in other deletion regimes. We release a role-matched evaluation protocol and an audit checklist for perturbation-based deletion claims.

</details>


### [124] [AIM: A Privacy-Aware Interoperable Memory Framework for Multi-Agent Multi-User LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12320)
*Zachary Johnson,Nigel Boachie Kumankumah,Somya Chatterjee,Tejas Sathyamurthi,Min Chen,Xinyi Alice Li,Xiao Wang,Emily Morgan Gelchie,Jessica Lin,Sadid A. Hasan,Sulaiman Vesal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AIM是一个隐私感知的多智能体、多用户记忆框架，可持久化管理私有与共享记忆，并配套提出首个多用户记忆基准MUMBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM局限于单用户会话，无法跨会话学习用户偏好；现有多智能体记忆系统多停留在单用户层面，限制了可跨用户共享的公共知识对下游响应的增益。

Method: AIM将信息动态分类为私有（仅限单个用户访问）或公共（所有用户可访问），并施加索引级访问控制，确保私有记忆仅所有者可检索；同时构建MUMBench，覆盖四类领域的多用户交互数据，包含私有与可共享信息。

Result: 在MUMBench上三次独立运行，AIM取得96.0%可见性分类准确率、58.8%严格操作准确率和70.5%状态感知操作准确率。

Conclusion: AIM在多用户环境下兼顾隐私保护与共享知识利用，MUMBench为多用户记忆操作（检索、创建、更新、删除）评估提供了首个公开基准。

Abstract: Traditional large language models (LLMs) are scoped to individual user sessions, limiting their knowledge to a single conversation and preventing them from learning user preferences that evolve over time. Existing agentic memory systems address this limitation but generally operate at the individual-user level, restricting the public knowledge that could be shared across users to improve downstream responses. We introduce AIM (Agentic Interoperable Memory), a unified, privacy-aware memory framework that enables multi-agent, multi-user LLM systems to persistently manage private and shared memory. AIM dynamically classifies information as private, scoped to one user and inaccessible to others, or public, accessible to all users. It enforces index-level access controls so that private memories are retrievable only by their owner, protecting sensitive data while allowing beneficial shared knowledge to improve coordination and consistency. We also introduce MUMBench (Multi-User Memory Benchmark), a dataset of multi-user interactions containing private and shareable information across four domains. To our knowledge, MUMBench is the first public dataset designed to evaluate multiple memory operations, including retrieval, creation, update, and deletion, in a multi-user environment. Across three independent runs on MUMBench, AIM achieves 96.0% visibility classification accuracy, 58.8% strict operation accuracy, and 70.5% state-aware operation accuracy.

</details>


### [125] [Affective Agent: On-Device Personalized Intervention Reasoning for Wearable Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12322)
*Reina Mun,Zishen Wan,Vijay Janapa Reddi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Affective Agent——一种面向可穿戴级硬件的三层参考架构，用于在不确定性下进行个性化干预推理，决定是否、何时以及如何干预，且无需云端依赖或逐用户重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感计算已推进可穿戴状态推断，但设备端关于“是否、何时、如何”干预的推理仍具挑战性；现有方案多依赖云端或逐用户重训练，难以在可穿戴级硬件上实现闭环个性化干预。

Method: 架构分三层：感知（perception）、个性化（personalization）、推理（reasoning）。结合亚十亿参数的紧凑语言模型与生理证据、上下文及用户历史进行决策；个性化通过主机管理结构化记忆的演化实现，而非逐用户权重更新。

Result: 在室内环境质量控制场景实例化，并在模拟器生成的留出纵向场景（涵盖生理变化、上下文、信号质量与干预历史）上评估：记忆驱动个性化与两遍结构化推理提升了干预决策质量（合成评估条件下）。

Conclusion: 将决策层迁移至设备端，展示了从可穿戴状态推断迈向可穿戴级硬件上闭环、个性化干预的可行路径。

Abstract: Affective computing has advanced wearable state inference, but on-device reasoning about whether, when, and how to intervene remains challenging. We present Affective Agent, a three-layer reference architecture for personalized intervention reasoning under uncertainty on wearable-class hardware. It combines a compact sub-billion-parameter language model with physiological evidence, context, and user history to decide whether, when, and how to intervene, without cloud dependency or per-user retraining. The architecture is organized into three interacting layers (perception, personalization, and reasoning), adapting to individual users through host-managed structured memory evolution rather than per-user weight updates. We instantiate Affective Agent in indoor environmental quality control and evaluate it on held-out, simulator-generated longitudinal scenarios spanning physiological variation, context, signal quality, and intervention history. Results show that memory-driven personalization and two-pass structured reasoning improve intervention decisions within this synthetic evaluation. By moving the decision layer on-device, this work demonstrates a path from wearable state inference toward closed-loop, personalized intervention on wearable-class hardware.

</details>


### [126] [LoRA-RC: Reservoir Computing with Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12327)
*Wenbin Wan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LoRA-RC，用低秩在线校正自适应储备池循环矩阵，并通过谱范数球投影保证收缩性与增量ISS稳定性，在漂移Lorenz系统上显著降低预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储备计算（RC）仅训练固定循环层上的线性读出，具有快速、数据高效、适合在线预测的优点。但静态储备池在系统漂移下性能退化，仅自适应读出不足以应对，而无约束的储备池自适应又会破坏回波状态性质与增量稳定性，从而损害RC可靠性。因此需要一种既能在线自适应又不破坏稳定性保证的方法。

Method: LoRA-RC通过由流式预测误差驱动的低秩校正来自适应循环矩阵。基础储备池与自适应用基离线固定，仅在线自适应一个小型核心矩阵，并在每一步将其投影到谱范数球上、进行低通滤波。该投影保证每个应用的循环矩阵始终位于可证收缩集合内，并沿每条在线自适应路径建立增量输入-状态稳定性（ISS）界，且速率与增益具有路径无关性。

Result: 在具有参数突变的Lorenz系统上，LoRA-RC相对固定RC将漂移后预测误差降低56%，相对仅自适应读出降低51%。基于20个随机种子的消融实验表明，去掉投影会使该误差膨胀超过40倍。

Conclusion: 低秩在线校正配合谱范数球投影，可在不牺牲RC稳定性保证的前提下实现储备池自适应，显著提升漂移场景下的在线预测精度；投影约束是维持稳定与性能的关键。

Abstract: Reservoir computing (RC) trains only a linear readout over a fixed recurrent layer, making it fast and data-efficient for online prediction. However, a static reservoir degrades under system drift, readout-only adaptation is then insufficient, and unconstrained reservoir adaptation can destroy the echo-state and incremental stability properties that make RC reliable. This paper proposes LoRA-RC, which adapts the recurrent matrix through a low-rank correction driven by streaming prediction errors. The base reservoir and adaptation bases are fixed offline; a small core matrix is adapted online, projected onto a spectral-norm ball, and low-pass filtered at each step. The projection guarantees that every applied recurrent matrix remains within a certified contraction set, and an incremental input-to-state stability bound is established for the reservoir along each online adaptation path, with path-independent rate and gain. On a Lorenz system with an abrupt parameter drift, LoRA-RC cuts post-drift prediction error by 56% versus a fixed RC and 51% versus readout-only adaptation; ablations over 20 seeds show that removing the projection inflates this error by more than a factor of 40.

</details>


### [127] [Toward Robust Personalized Alignment for LLMs: Mitigating Persona Drift in Multi-Turn Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12373)
*Youyuan Zhang,Siyuan Li,Fangming Liu,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CORE框架，将回合局部证据与持久人格状态修订分离，通过不确定性感知的信念修正选择性更新用户偏好，并构建PERSIST基准评测人格状态鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化语言模型面临「人格漂移」问题：用户画像随长期交互演化而非固定不变。模型需在偏好真正改变时修正持久人格状态，同时避免被短暂、模糊或未解决的观察所误导而更新。

Method: CORE：区分回合局部证据与持久人格状态的修订，采用不确定性感知的信念修正机制，对有理据的用户偏好进行选择性更新。并引入PERSIST——一个留出的、锚点后基准，用于在顺序交互压力下评测人格状态鲁棒性，涵盖歧义、冲突与受控社会影响三类情形。

Result: 在ALOE、PersonaChat与PERSIST上，CORE提升了个性化对齐度与鲁棒性，在归一化闭槽状态保真度上取得互补收益。人类评估与机制性控制实验进一步表明，其显式更新控制能力超越了单纯更强的生成能力或持久记忆方案。

Conclusion: 显式的、基于不确定性感知的信念修正机制是应对人格漂移的有效途径，且独立于生成能力与记忆机制的提升，为个性化语言模型的人格状态维护提供了新方向。

Abstract: Persona drift remains a central challenge for personalized language models, as user profiles evolve over long interactions rather than remain permanently fixed. Models must therefore revise persistent persona states when preferences genuinely change, while avoiding updates driven by transient, ambiguous, or unresolved observations. We propose CORE, which separates turn-local evidence from persistent persona-state revision and selectively updates grounded user preferences through uncertainty-aware belief revision. We also introduce PERSIST, a held-out post-anchor benchmark for persona-state robustness under sequential interaction stress, covering ambiguity, conflict, and controlled social influence. Across ALOE, PersonaChat, and PERSIST, CORE improves personalized alignment and robustness, with complementary gains in normalized closed-slot state fidelity. Human evaluation and mechanistic controls further support explicit update control beyond stronger generation or persistent memory alone.

</details>


### [128] [BlueLM-GUI Technical Report: A Real-Device-Centric Flywheel for Self-Improving Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12394)
*Tong Ye,Kunyang Han,Guozhi Wang,Longqiang Luo,Zhifeng Ding,Yongxiang Zhang,Xiaolei Shen,Yuxuan Zhang,Zhuping Zhang,Tao Xu,Yue Pan,Yucheng Zhao,Yupei Hu,Yuanjiang Ouyang,Danfeng Shen,Runqi Lin,Hongda Cai,Zhaoxiong Wang,Mengjia Yan,Yingjie Zhong,Chen Zhou,Zeyu Zhang,Xuwen Zhu,Penggang Shi,Mingcheng Luo,Ziyang Wu,Min Jin,Mingfu Shen,Zairong Xu,Fan Zhang,Hao Wang,Liang Liu,Zhulin Xie,Lijun Yao,Xiao Liang,Liangmin Wen,Liqiang Feng,Feilong Wu,Min Hu,Min Chen,Guanjing Xiong,Xiaohu Ruan,Xiaoxin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BlueLM-GUI是一个35B-A3B原生端到端移动GUI智能体，以真实设备为中心构建"数据-训练-评估"飞轮，在MobileGUI-VBench上取得87.4分（超越最佳闭源模型5.1分），在AndroidWorld上取得84.9分（开源最优）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级移动GUI智能体部署面临三大持续存在的差距：1）沙盒训练与生产环境之间存在分布不匹配；2）昂贵的真机失败数据未被充分利用；3）固定基准测试趋于饱和，丧失指导迭代的能力。

Method: 提出以真实设备为中心的飞轮框架，遵循三个原则：Every Sample Matters——双轨流水线结合异构三元系统共识评估（Heterogeneous Triple-System Consensus）与错误纠正及派生模块（Error Correction & Derivation Module），将每条轨迹转化为可用监督信号；Every Rollout Is Real——三阶段配方（持续预训练、监督微调、数百台真机上的智能体强化学习）使每次rollout扎根真实生产环境；Every Query Evolves——基于配额驱动的基准方法论，通过三个正交轴实现精确归因并支持基准随模型能力提升而系统升级。

Result: 在MobileGUI-VBench上达到87.4分，超越最佳闭源模型5.1分；在AndroidWorld上达到84.9分，为开源模型最佳结果，与闭源模型具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 将模型训练与迭代改进扎根于真实设备与三大"Every"原则，可获得强大、鲁棒且可迁移的移动GUI能力。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents are shifting from multi-module frameworks to native models trained end-to-end, yet industrial deployment faces three persistent gaps. Sandbox training produces a distribution mismatch with production environments; expensive real-device failures remain underutilized; and fixed benchmarks saturate, losing the power to guide iteration. We present BlueLM-GUI, a 35B-A3B mobile GUI agent built as a real-device-centric flywheel that closes these gaps through three principles. Every Sample Matters: a dual-track pipeline with Heterogeneous Triple-System Consensus evaluation and an Error Correction \& Derivation Module salvages every trajectory into usable supervision. Every Rollout Is Real: a three-stage recipe---continual pre-training, supervised fine-tuning, and agentic reinforcement learning on hundreds of real phones---grounds every rollout in real production environments, so the capability the model learns transfers directly to deployment. Every Query Evolves: a quota-driven benchmark methodology with three orthogonal axes enables precise attribution and allows the benchmark to be systematically upgraded as the model improves. BlueLM-GUI achieves 87.4 on MobileGUI-VBench, surpassing the best closed-source model by 5.1 points, and 84.9 on AndroidWorld, the best result among open-source models and competitive with closed-source models. These results demonstrate that grounding model training and iterative improvement in both real devices and the three Every principles yields strong, robust, and transferable mobile GUI capability.

</details>


### [129] [Is Gaussian Splatting Becoming Neural Again? A Taxonomy and Controlled Study of Learned Parameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12395)
*YuanHang Wang,Xin Cao,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文梳理3DGS中"神经化"趋势，沿五个维度分类19种代表性方法，并通过受控实验表明：选择性神经化（共享外观与不透明度）有效，而解码几何结构无额外收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS结合显式基元与高效光栅化，但近期系统越来越多地用神经网络生成或共享高斯参数；作者希望厘清这一趋势，检验"神经化"是否能被简化为二元标签，并判断哪些神经化选择真正有益。

Method: 沿五个维度刻画该趋势：属性解码、空间共享、视角条件解码、拓扑生成、摊销推断（amortized inference）；对19种代表性方法进行归类分析；并在mip-NeRF 360上设计受控实验，分离出三种神经参数化形式进行对比。

Result: 对19种方法的分析显示，各类选择针对不同局限性，无法归结为二元的"神经/非神经"标签；受控实验表明，共享外观与不透明度可提升重建质量，而解码几何结构没有带来进一步增益。

Conclusion: 证据支持"选择性神经化"：当共享函数能捕捉可复用相关性、且不牺牲显式splat的局部几何自由度时，才应引入神经化。

Abstract: Three-dimensional Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) combines explicit primitives with efficient rasterization, yet recent systems increasingly use neural networks to generate or share Gaussian parameters. We characterize this trend along five axes: attribute decoding, spatial sharing, view-conditioned decoding, topology generation, and amortized inference. An analysis of 19 representative methods shows that these choices address different limitations and cannot be reduced to a binary neural label. We also isolate three forms of neural parameterization in a controlled mip-NeRF 360 study. Sharing appearance and opacity improves reconstruction quality, while decoding geometric structure offers no further gain. The evidence favors selective neuralization: shared functions help when they capture reusable correlations without sacrificing the local geometric freedom of explicit splats.

</details>


### [130] [Niching Agents in The Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12398)
*Gary B. Parker,Jim O'Connor,John Asaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了The Core竞争性协同进化算法，通过将智能体生态位化（niching）到Xpilot游戏环境的特定子集，实验表明受限子环境中进化的智能体在各自子环境中的成功率优于针对整个系统或不同子环境进化的智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The Core是一种无需传统适应度函数的竞争性协同进化算法，此前已证明其能在Xpilot游戏中进化出具备战斗与导航能力的智能体；本文动机在于探究将智能体生态位化到原始环境的特定子集后，能否获得比面向整个系统更强的专化控制能力。

Method: 沿用The Core框架，智能体通过局部交互、锦标赛选择、交叉与变异产生后代，从而进化出更优控制器；在此基础上引入生态位划分，将智能体分别限制在原始Xpilot环境的特定子环境中进行进化，并与面向整个系统及面向不同子环境的智能体进行对比。

Result: 实验结果显示，生态位化到特定子环境的智能体在该子环境中取得的成功率，高于面向整个系统进化的智能体，也高于生态位化到其他子环境进化的智能体。

Conclusion: 将竞争性协同进化智能体限制于特定子环境可提升其在该环境中的专化性能，说明生态位划分是增强The Core进化控制器环境适应性与有效性的可行手段。

Abstract: The Core is a unique competitive co-evolution algorithm that allows agents to evolve autonomous control without utilizing a traditional fitness function. The agents evolve via local interactions through tournament selection, crossover, and mutation, producing offspring by evolving better controllers. Previous works have shown The Core's ability to evolve agents capable of combat and navigation in the Xpilot video game. This research expands upon that premise by niching agents to specific subsets of the original environment The Core was tested in. Our results demonstrate the niched agents capacity for success over agents niched to the entire system and agents niched to different sub-environments.

</details>


### [131] [OneLA: Scaling Linear-Attention Decoding to Large Beams in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12399)
*Xiangrui Yang,Cheng Peng,Yunfeng Zhao,Liang Zeng,Ao Hu,Jiawei Yang,Shengzhe Wang,Jingshan Lv,Xiao Liang,Chen Yang,Jiaqiang Liu,Yiming Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OneLA：利用生成式推荐中共享前缀与短发散后缀特性的线性注意力解码框架，通过共享状态+增量记录表示所有beam状态，实现1.54-2.46倍端到端解码加速并大幅降低内存与数据搬运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐依赖大beam解码生成数百候选项，给循环式线性注意力带来新的扩展性挑战。现有线性注意力服务系统要么为每个beam实体化完整循环状态，要么反复重放共享历史，造成显著的内存与流量开销。

Method: OneLA用单一由prompt派生的共享状态，加上各beam发散转移的紧凑、仅追加记录来表示所有beam状态；每个解码步仅计算所需状态信息，不重建每个beam的完整循环状态。采用轻量级祖先索引追踪构成各beam历史的转移记录，使beam更新无需移动或复制既有记录。并设计融合GPU内核跨beam复用共享状态。

Result: 实现1.54-2.46倍端到端解码加速，同时显著降低循环状态内存占用与数据搬运量。

Conclusion: 通过挖掘GR工作负载中共享prompt与短发散后缀的结构特性，OneLA在避免完整状态实体化和历史重放的前提下高效服务大beam线性注意力解码。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) relies on large-beam decoding to generate hundreds of candidate items, creating a new scaling challenge for recurrent linear attention. Existing linear attention serving systems either materialize a full recurrent state for every beam or repeatedly replay shared history, incurring substantial memory and traffic overhead. To address this, we present OneLA, a linear-attention decoding framework that exploits the shared prompt and short divergent suffixes of GR workloads. Specifically, OneLA represents all beam states using a single shared prompt-derived state and compact, append-only records of their divergent transitions. Using this representation, OneLA computes only the state information required at each decoding step, without reconstructing a full recurrent state for every beam. Furthermore, OneLA uses a lightweight ancestry index to track the transition records that make up each beam's history, allowing beams to be updated without moving or copying existing records. A fused GPU kernel further reuses the shared state across beams. Our analysis shows that OneLA achieves 1.54-2.46x end-to-end decode speedups while substantially reducing recurrent-state memory use and data movement.

</details>


### [132] [Decentralized Evolution of Hexapod Gaits with Independent Leg Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12400)
*Gary B. Parker,John Asaro,Jim O'Connor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种去中心化进化算法，独立进化六足机器人每条腿的步态控制器，无需集中协调即可涌现出高效协调的运动行为，并在Webots仿真中优于协作协同进化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六足机器人步态优化通常依赖集中式协调，复杂度高、难以扩展。作者希望探索去中心化策略，降低步态优化复杂度，实现可扩展、自适应的机器人运动控制。

Method: 基于Webots仿真器和Mantis六足机器人，采用去中心化进化算法对每条腿的控制器进行独立进化，不施加集中协调机制，让协调行为自然涌现；并与协作协同进化（cooperative coevolution）方法进行基准对比。

Result: 去中心化方法在生成稳定且自适应的步态方面表现出更高的有效性，同时展现出有趣的涌现式协调行为，优于协作协同进化基准。

Conclusion: 通过独立进化腿部控制器可降低步态优化复杂度，凸显了去中心化策略在构建可扩展、自适应机器人系统方面的潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach to hexapod locomotion by evolving each leg's gait independently through a decentralized evolutionary algorithm. Using the Webots simulator and the Mantis hexapod robot, we optimize individual leg controllers without centralized coordination, allowing emergent behaviors to drive the development of efficient, coordinated locomotion. Our decentralized method is benchmarked against cooperative coevolution, demonstrating improved efficacy in generating stable and adaptive gaits while showing interesting emergent coordination. By enabling independent evolution of leg controllers, this method reduces the complexity of gait optimization and highlights the potential of decentralized strategies for scalable and adaptive robotic systems.

</details>


### [133] [Beyond ID Embeddings: Process-Grounded Language Modeling for Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12403)
*Minghang Liu,Yuanzhuo Wang,Qiang Qiu,Huawei Shen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出过程感知语言认知诊断框架PLCD，用语言结构作认知先验、作答记录校准学生后验状态，提升预测性能与冷启动鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知诊断模型依赖离散的、基于ID的嵌入来表示学生、习题和概念，这与学习者认知的本质不符——知识并非以孤立符号形式存储和提取。因此，当出现新习题或新概念时，CDM存在语义局限性（冷启动问题）。

Method: PLCD框架：1）利用大语言模型构建概念图式（concept schemas）和认知过程图（cognitive process graphs）；2）采用目标条件语义记忆（target-conditioned semantic memory）检索与每个目标习题相关的历史作答；3）设计过程锚定的Language-to-Cognition Mapper，结合DA-MoE专家与过程级对比学习，将文本证据映射到统一认知空间。

Result: 实验表明，PLCD在学生表现预测上优于传统基线，并展现出较强的认知迁移能力；结构化语言先验经作答记录校准后，可提升冷启动鲁棒性与认知扎根性。

Conclusion: 该工作将大语言模型的计算能力与心理测量学测量潜在知识状态的目标相连接，表明以语言派生结构作认知先验、以作答记录校准的范式可行且有效。

Abstract: Cognitive Diagnosis Models (CDMs) play a pivotal role in personalized online learning. Traditional CDMs rely on discrete, ID-based embeddings to represent students, exercises, and concepts. This paradigm diverges from the nature of learner cognition, where knowledge is not stored and retrieved as isolated symbols. As a result, CDMs suffer from semantic limitations when new exercises or concepts appear. In this paper, we propose a Process-aware Language Cognitive Diagnosis (PLCD) framework that uses language-derived structures as cognitive priors and response records to calibrate student posterior states. PLCD leverages large language models (LLMs) to construct concept schemas and cognitive process graphs, and uses target-conditioned semantic memory to retrieve historical responses that are relevant to each target exercise. A process-grounded Language-to-Cognition Mapper with DA-MoE experts and process-level contrastive learning then maps the textual evidence into a unified cognitive space. Experimental results show that PLCD not only outperforms traditional baselines in predicting student performance but also exhibits strong cognitive transfer capabilities. These results connect the computational power of LLMs with the psychometric goal of measuring latent knowledge states, suggesting that structured language priors calibrated by response records can improve cold-start robustness and cognitive grounding.

</details>


### [134] [VRL-Bench: Benchmarking agents on computer control tasks under finite trial budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12404)
*Yu Bai,Yukai Miao,Dawei Wang,Li Chen,Yanyu Ren,Yuqian Shi,Dan Li,Ying Xiong,Chengqiu Tan,Run Zhou,Li Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VRL-Bench用于在有限试验预算下公平评测"试错学习"类语言智能体，并发现常用言语记忆方法（如Reflexion）存在利用经验与持续探索的权衡，进而提出VEX²调度器以联合选择策略与分配剩余试验预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体在计算机控制等复杂任务上可通过试错学习改进，但现有言语强化学习方法（如Reflexion）缺乏在有限试验预算下的公平评测，且其效果不稳定。

Method: 构建VRL-Bench评测框架；在MiniWoB和WebShop上、对三种模型，评估多种言语记忆方法的更新效果；通过replay实验分析反思的影响；提出VEX²——用语言模型联合选择策略并分配剩余试验预算的言语探索-利用调度器。

Result: 各言语记忆方法仅在部分设置中优于无记忆重试，部分设置反而更差；replay实验显示反思可能降低成功率；VEX²是唯一在全部六个设置中都取得正向成功率增益的更新方法。

Conclusion: 试错学习中的言语记忆方法存在利用经验与持续探索的权衡，VEX²通过显式调度探索-利用实现了稳定的性能提升。

Abstract: Learning from trial and error is a promising way to improve language agents on complex tasks such as computer control. Reflexion introduced verbal reinforcement learning, which turns failed trials into text that guides later attempts without updating model parameters. We introduce VRL-Bench, a harness for fair evaluation of trial-and-error learning under finite trial budgets. Across three models on MiniWoB and WebShop, we evaluate updates from several prominent verbal-memory methods spanning Reflexion and later work: each improves observed success over memory-free retry in some settings but reduces it in others. Replay experiments show that using reflection can reduce success rates, revealing a trade-off between exploiting experience and continued exploration. We propose VEX$^2$, a verbal exploration--exploitation scheduler that uses a language model to jointly select policies and allocate the remaining trial budget. VEX$^2$ is the only evaluated update to achieve positive observed success-rate gains over retry in all six settings.

</details>


### [135] [SoK: Rethinking Jailbreaking in the Era of Agentic AI: Attacks, Defenses, and Practical Consideration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12413)
*Md Jueal Mia,Yanzhao Wu,Selcuk Uluagac,M. Hadi Amini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇SoK（知识系统化）研究，将越狱安全重置于完整智能体执行流水线之上，构建攻防统一分类法与安全-效用-效率评估框架，经实证研究发现原生对齐强≠抗越狱、防御效果高度依赖情境、低最终攻击成功率可能掩盖严重中间环节失陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正从对话助手演变为具备推理、规划、工具调用、持久记忆、多智能体通信与多步任务执行能力的智能体系统；同时现代模型的原生安全对齐显著强于早期模型，而既有越狱攻防研究多基于旧世代模型。因此需要回答：哪些既有越狱安全结论在现代LLM与智能体AI时代仍然成立。

Method: 采用Systematization of Knowledge（SoK）方法，围绕完整智能体执行流水线重构越狱安全问题：(1) 构建覆盖用户交互、规划推理、记忆、工具使用、智能体间通信的统一攻击与防御分类法；(2) 提出安全-效用-效率评估框架，区分原生有害提示安全性、对抗性越狱鲁棒性与智能体层面安全结果；(3) 在统一智能体框架下对代表性攻防开展受控实证研究。

Result: 揭示三个重要缺口：其一，强原生对齐并不意味着对对抗性越狱的鲁棒性；其二，防御有效性高度依赖模型、攻击方式与组件，且可能以过度拒答、效用损失和延迟为显著代价；其三，较低的最终响应攻击成功率可能掩盖严重的中间环节失陷——即使最终响应被成功过滤，规划、记忆与工具交互仍可能不安全。

Conclusion: 研究结论主张越狱防御范式应从以响应为中心转向跨层、执行感知的安全，即保护智能体状态、组件间转换与外部动作，同时兼顾实际效用与效率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly evolving from conversational assistants into agentic AI systems that reason, plan, invoke tools, maintain persistent memory, communicate with other agents, and execute multi-step tasks. At the same time, modern models exhibit substantially stronger native safety alignment than earlier generations on which many jailbreak attacks and defenses were originally studied. This shift raises a fundamental question: \textit{which established jailbreak-security findings remain valid in the era of modern LLMs and agentic AI?} We address this question through a Systematization of Knowledge (SoK) that reframes jailbreak security around the full agentic execution pipeline. We develop unified taxonomies of attacks and defenses spanning user interaction, planning and reasoning, memory, tool use, and inter-agent communication, and introduce a security--utility--efficiency evaluation framework that separates native harmful-prompt safety, adversarial jailbreak robustness, and agent-level security outcomes. We further conduct a controlled empirical study of representative attacks and defenses within a common agentic framework. Our results reveal three important gaps. First, strong native alignment does not imply robustness to adversarial jailbreaks. Second, defense effectiveness is highly model-, attack-, and component-dependent and can come at substantial cost in over-refusal, utility, and latency. Third, low final-response attack success can mask severe intermediate compromise: planning, memory, and tool interactions may remain unsafe even when the final response is successfully filtered. These findings motivate a shift from response-centric jailbreak defense toward cross-layer, execution-aware security that protects agent state, component transitions, and external actions while preserving practical utility and efficiency.

</details>


### [136] [Hierarchical Belief Modeling for Zero-Shot Opponent Adaptation in Partially Observable Multi-Agent Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12422)
*Kowei Shih,Lu Cheng,Zeyu Wang,Yeyun Xu,Kejian Tong*

Main category: cs.AI
